PB001 ⏱4h · 📈+8.6%

診断 h2 直下の段落構造が不在 / 質問形式のクエリに直接答えていない
アルゴリズム BM25 + Featured Snippet Paragraph extraction (Google internal)
UI/Schema h2 で質問 → 直下 <p> 40-60 字回答 → 詳細展開。Schema.org FAQPage or Article
<h2>○○とは</h2>
<p>○○とは、〜である。</p>
<!-- 40-60字以内、最初の文に答えを置く -->
KPI Featured Snippet 獲得数 / Position 0 impression / SERP CTR Δ
推奨ツール Ahrefs SERP Features Report / SEMrush / Google Search Console
PB002 ⏱8h · 📈+15.0%

アルゴリズム Critical CSS Extraction + LCP attribution (web-vitals JS)
UI/Schema Hero <img> に loading='eager' fetchpriority='high' / <link rel='preload' as='image'> / WebP/AVIF 配信 / Critical CSS inline / 非クリティカル CSS は async load
<link rel="preload" as="image" href="/hero.webp" fetchpriority="high">
<img src="/hero.webp" loading="eager" fetchpriority="high" alt="...">
KPI LCP p75 (mobile) / CrUX LCP good URLs % / PageSpeed Insights score
推奨ツール PageSpeed Insights / Lighthouse / WebPageTest / web-vitals JS
PB003 ⏱6h · 📈+10.0%

診断 画像/iframe の width/height 未指定 / 広告 reservation 不足 / 動的コンテンツ挿入
アルゴリズム Layout Shift attribution (PerformanceObserver layout-shift)
UI/Schema <img width height> 必須 / aspect-ratio CSS / 広告枠は min-height 予約 / web font は font-display: optional
<img src="..." width="1200" height="630" alt="...">
<style>img { aspect-ratio: 16/9; } @font-face { font-display: optional; }</style>
KPI CLS p75 / CrUX CLS good URLs %
推奨ツール PageSpeed Insights / Chrome DevTools Performance

PB007 ⏱24h · 📈+18.0%

Hybrid Search (BM25+Dense) で BM25 単独より精度低下

診断 BM25 と Dense のスコア正規化が不適切 / RRF を使っていない
アルゴリズム Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF, k=60)
UI/Schema BM25 top-100 + Dense top-100 → RRF 統合 → top-10 表示
function hybrid(q):
  bm25_ranked = bm25.search(q, k=100)
  dense_ranked = dense.search(q, k=100)
  return RRF([bm25_ranked, dense_ranked], k=60)[:10]
KPI MRR@10 / NDCG@10 / 既存 Search Analytics で対照群比較
推奨ツール Elasticsearch + sentence-transformers / Vespa / Weaviate

PB019 ⏱40h · 📈+20.0%

ColBERT/Dense Retrieval を導入したいが index size が爆発

診断 1 doc に 100+ token embeddings → corpus * dim 巨大
アルゴリズム ColBERT Late Interaction + Product Quantization
UI/Schema ColBERTv2 + token embedding compression (PQ) / 各 doc を 100 token に切る / FAISS IVF+PQ
from colbert import Indexer
indexer = Indexer(checkpoint='colbertv2', config=ColBERTConfig(nbits=2, kmeans_niters=4))
indexer.index(name='my-index', collection=docs)
KPI Index size GB / MRR@10 / Throughput QPS
推奨ツール ColBERTv2 (Stanford) / FAISS / Vespa
PB020 ⏱8h · 📈+90.0%

Spell correction が遅い、Lucene FuzzyQuery で latency >100ms

診断 BK-tree / Levenshtein automaton で文字列空間を線形探索
アルゴリズム SymSpell (Wolf Garbe 2012)
UI/Schema SymSpell index (delete-only) を build / Redis にキャッシュ / 編集距離2まで対応
from symspellpy import SymSpell
sym = SymSpell(max_dictionary_edit_distance=2)
sym.load_dictionary('dict.txt', term_index=0, count_index=1)
results = sym.lookup('helo wrld', verbosity=2, max_edit_distance=2)
KPI Suggest latency p95 / Suggestion accuracy / Throughput
推奨ツール SymSpell (GitHub: wolfgarbe/SymSpell) / Redis
PB021 ⏱24h · 📈+30.0%

A/B テストが終わらない、サンプルが集まらない

診断 固定 sample size の Frequentist A/B は CTR 差が小さいと長期化
アルゴリズム Thompson Sampling Multi-Armed Bandit
UI/Schema Bandit allocator (Optimizely Bandit / VWO Bandit / 自作) で動的に勝者寄せ
import random
alpha, beta = [1]*n_arms, [1]*n_arms
for t in range(n_rounds):
  samples = [random.betavariate(alpha[a], beta[a]) for a in range(n_arms)]
  chosen = samples.index(max(samples))
  reward = pull(chosen)
  if reward: alpha[chosen]+=1
  else: beta[chosen]+=1
KPI Test convergence time / Regret / Winner confidence
推奨ツール Optimizely / VWO / Statsig / Eppo / GrowthBook
PB022 ⏱4h · 📈+0%

コンテンツ評価で評価者間の一致が κ=0.65 (Substantial 止まり)

診断 高有病率 (90% verified) のため Cohen's Kappa がパラドックスで過小評価
アルゴリズム Gwet's AC1 (rule:quality-metrics-standard.md 主指標)
UI/Schema 5 指標セット必須: observed_agreement / gwet_ac1 / cohens_kappa / positive_prevalence / sample_size
python3 ~/projects/research/agent-registry-db/tools/gwet_ac1.py rater1.csv rater2.csv
# Output: AC1=0.943 (Almost Perfect), κ=0.821 (Substantial)
KPI Gwet AC1 / Po / Cohen's Kappa / Sample size
推奨ツール agent-registry-db/tools/gwet_ac1.py / sklearn cohen_kappa_score

Author Authority Score

著者権威スコア content_eeat ranking 2018

E-E-A-T の Authoritativeness を Schema.org Person + 外部識別子で定量化。YMYL コンテンツで決定的。

$$A(\mathrm{author}) = w_o \cdot \mathbb{I}[\mathrm{ORCID}] + w_w \cdot \mathbb{I}[\mathrm{QID}] + w_c \log(1+c) + w_s \cdot \mathrm{quality}(\mathrm{sameAs})$$
Time O(|sameAs|) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function author_authority(author):
  signals = {
    'orcid': 0.2 if author.orcid else 0,
    'wikidata': 0.25 if author.wikidata_qid else 0,
    'institution_rank': institutional_score(author.affiliation),
    'citations': log10(1 + author.citation_count) / 5,
    'sameAs_count': min(len(author.sameAs) / 10, 0.3),
    'sameAs_quality': sum(domain_authority(url) for url in author.sameAs) / 100,
  }
  return sum(signals.values())
入力
  • author_profile: Person — Schema.org Person with sameAs
出力
  • score: float — 0..1 authority composite
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

Content Decay Detection

コンテンツ崩壊検出 content_eeat evaluation 2018

GSC データから 30日窓 vs 150日前ベースラインの click ratio で崩壊検出。30%超で refresh トリガー。

$$\text{DecayRate}(u, T) = \frac{\bar{C}_{T-30:T} - \bar{C}_{T-180:T-150}}{\bar{C}_{T-180:T-150}}, \quad \text{trigger refresh if } > 0.3$$
Time O(lookback) Space O(lookback)
擬似コード
function detect_decay(url, lookback_days=180):
  history = fetch_gsc_data(url, days=lookback_days)
  current_traffic = mean(history[-30:].clicks)
  baseline_traffic = mean(history[-180:-150].clicks)
  decay_rate = (baseline_traffic - current_traffic) / baseline_traffic
  if decay_rate > 0.3:
    return {'status': 'decaying', 'rate': decay_rate, 'action': 'refresh_recommended'}
  return {'status': 'stable', 'rate': decay_rate}
入力
  • url: str —
  • gsc_api: GSCClient —
出力
  • decay_report: Dict —
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

E-E-A-T Composite Score

E-E-A-T 複合スコア content_eeat ranking 2022

Quality Rater Guidelines 2022年12月版から Experience を追加した E-E-A-T。各軸の評価器を統合。

$$EEAT = 0.2 E_x + 0.3 E_p + 0.3 A + 0.2 T$$
Time O(|signals|) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function eeat_composite(page, author, site):
  experience = signals_from_first_person_account(page)
  expertise = author_credentials_score(author) + topic_match(author.knowsAbout, page.topic)
  authoritativeness = backlinks_from_authority(site) + wikidata_present(site)
  trust = has_about_page(site) + has_contact(site) + https_present(site) + medical_review(page)
  return 0.2*experience + 0.3*expertise + 0.3*authoritativeness + 0.2*trust
入力
  • page: Document —
  • author: Person —
  • site: Site —
出力
  • score: float — 0..1
Bench —

First-Person Experience Detector

一人称体験検出 content_eeat evaluation 2022

Google 2022年12月の Experience 軸 (E-E-A-T 第2の E) を一人称代名詞・固有日付・独自画像で定量化。

$$E_x(t) = 0.3 \cdot \min(\frac{n_{1pp}}{20}, 1) + 0.2 \cdot \min(\frac{n_{date}}{5}, 1) + 0.2 \cdot \min(\frac{n_{img}}{3}, 1) + 0.2 \cdot \min(\frac{n_{num}}{10}, 1) + 0.1 \cdot \mathbb{I}[\text{time-marker}]$$
Time O(|text|) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function detect_experience_signals(text):
  # Detect: 'I used', 'we tested', 'our results', personal photos, dates
  signals = {
    'first_person_count': count_regex(text, r'(I|we|me|our|my)'),
    'personal_dates': len(extract_specific_dates(text)),
    'original_images': count_unique_images_not_stock(text),
    'specific_numbers': count_regex(text, r'\d+\.\d+|\$\d+|\d+%'),
    'time_marker': has_phrases(text, ['I spent', 'after testing', 'in my experience'])
  }
  experience_score = (
    min(signals['first_person_count'] / 20, 1) * 0.3 +
    min(signals['personal_dates'] / 5, 1) * 0.2 +
    min(signals['original_images'] / 3, 1) * 0.2 +
    min(signals['specific_numbers'] / 10, 1) * 0.2 +
    (0.1 if signals['time_marker'] else 0)
  )
  return experience_score
入力
  • text: str —
出力
  • score: float —
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

Helpful Content Classifier (HCS)

Helpful Content 分類器 content_eeat ranking 2022

Google 2022年8月導入の sitewide quality classifier。2024年 March Core Update で内部統合。

$$\text{HCS}(p) = \sum_i w_i \cdot \mathbb{I}[s^+_i(p)] - \sum_j v_j \cdot \mathbb{I}[s^-_j(p)] \cdot 1.5$$
Time O(|page|) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function HCS_score(page):
  features = {
    'people_first': len(experience_signals(page)) > 3,
    'expertise_demonstrated': has_author_bio_with_credentials(page),
    'original_research': contains_original_data(page),
    'thin_content': page.word_count < 300 and page.has_affiliate_links,
    'auto_generated': llm_detection_score(page.text) > 0.7,
    'misleading_title': clickbait_classifier(page.title) > 0.7,
  }
  positive = sum([features['people_first'], features['expertise_demonstrated'], features['original_research']])
  negative = sum([features['thin_content'], features['auto_generated'], features['misleading_title']])
  return positive - negative * 1.5
入力
  • page: Document —
出力
  • hcs_score: float —
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

Originality Detector (Paraphrase + Plagiarism)

オリジナリティ検出 content_eeat evaluation 2023

盗用 + LLM 生成 + SERP 上位類似度を統合したオリジナリティ評価。Helpful Content Update の中核検出ロジック。

$$O(t) = 1.0 - 0.5 \cdot P_{plag}(t) - 0.3 \cdot P_{LLM}(t) - 0.2 \cdot \max_{p' \in \text{SERP}_{10}} \cos(\vec{t}, \vec{p'})$$
Time O(|text| + |serp|*|text|) Space O(|text|)
擬似コード
function originality_score(text):
  # 1. Plagiarism check against web corpus
  plagiarism = check_copyleaks_or_grammarly(text)
  # 2. LLM-generated content detection
  llm_detected = gptzero_or_originality_ai_score(text)
  # 3. Embedding similarity to top-10 SERP results
  query = extract_main_topic(text)
  top_serp = serp_api.fetch(query)[:10]
  text_emb = encode(text)
  similarities = [cosine(text_emb, encode(p.text)) for p in top_serp]
  max_sim = max(similarities)
  return {
    'plagiarism_pct': plagiarism,
    'llm_score': llm_detected,
    'serp_similarity': max_sim,
    'originality': 1.0 - 0.5*plagiarism - 0.3*llm_detected - 0.2*max_sim
  }
入力
  • text: str —
出力
  • originality_report: Dict —
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

Pillar-Cluster Internal Link Optimizer

Pillar-Cluster 内部リンク最適化 content_eeat ranking 2016

HubSpot (2016) の Pillar-Cluster モデル。Hub-and-Spoke で内部 PageRank flow を Pillar に集中。

$$\text{score}(p_{cluster} \to p_{pillar}) = 1.0, \quad \text{score}(p_{pillar} \to p_{cluster}) = 0.7, \quad \forall t(p_{cluster}) = t(p_{pillar})$$
Time O(|cluster|) Space O(|cluster|)
擬似コード
function optimize_internal_links(pages, pillar_url):
  for cluster_page in pages:
    if cluster_page.topic == pillar_url.topic:
      # Each cluster page must link to pillar with descriptive anchor
      if pillar_url not in cluster_page.outlinks:
        cluster_page.add_link(pillar_url, anchor=pillar_url.title)
      # Pillar page should link to all cluster pages
      if cluster_page.url not in pillar_url.outlinks:
        pillar_url.add_link(cluster_page.url, anchor=cluster_page.title)
  return pages
入力
  • pages: List[Page] —
  • pillar: Page —
出力
  • updated_pages: List[Page] —
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

Schema.org JSON-LD @graph Builder

Schema.org @graph 構築 content_eeat indexing 2014

JSON-LD 1.1 @graph で複数エンティティ (Organization + WebSite + Article + BreadcrumbList) を1ブロックに集約。Knowledge Graph 候補化の鍵。

$$\mathcal{G} = \langle \mathcal{N}, \mathcal{E} \rangle, \quad \mathcal{N} = \{n_{org}, n_{site}, n_{article}, n_{breadcrumb}\}, \quad \mathcal{E} \subseteq \mathcal{N} \times \text{property} \times \mathcal{N}$$
Time O(|nodes|) Space O(|nodes|)
擬似コード
function build_schema_graph(page_meta):
  graph = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@graph": [
      {
        "@type": "Organization",
        "@id": page_meta.org_url + "#org",
        "name": page_meta.org_name,
        "url": page_meta.org_url,
        "logo": page_meta.logo_url,
        "sameAs": page_meta.sameAs_list  # wikipedia, linkedin, etc
      },
      {
        "@type": "WebSite",
        "@id": page_meta.site_url + "#site",
        "url": page_meta.site_url,
        "name": page_meta.site_name,
        "publisher": {"@id": page_meta.org_url + "#org"}
      },
      {
        "@type": "Article",
        "@id": page_meta.url + "#article",
        "headline": page_meta.title,
        "datePublished": page_meta.published,
        "dateModified": page_meta.modified,
        "author": {"@type": "Person", "name": page_meta.author},
        "publisher": {"@id": page_meta.org_url + "#org"},
        "mainEntityOfPage": page_meta.url
      },
      {
        "@type": "BreadcrumbList",
        "itemListElement": [
          {"@type": "ListItem", "position": i+1, "name": b.name, "item": b.url}
          for i, b in enumerate(page_meta.breadcrumbs)
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
  return json.dumps(graph, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
入力
  • page_meta: PageMetadata —
出力
  • jsonld: str —
Bench Google Rich Results Test — Google Rich Results compliance
📄 参考実装/論文

Topical Authority Score

トピカル権威スコア content_eeat ranking 2018

Pillar-Cluster + 内外リンク密度 + ランキング占有率からトピック権威を算出。Helpful Content Update 以降の中核。

$$TA(d, t) = \sum_i w_i \cdot \phi_i(d, t)$$
Time O(|pages| + |links|) Space O(|cluster|)
擬似コード
function topical_authority(domain, topic):
  cluster_pages = count_pages(domain, topic_cluster=topic)
  pillar_present = has_pillar_page(domain, topic)
  internal_links = count_internal_links(domain, topic_cluster=topic)
  external_authority = sum(da(link.source) for link in backlinks(domain, topic))
  ranking_share = sum(1 for q in topic_queries if rank(domain, q) <= 10)
  return weighted_sum({
    'cluster_size': log(1 + cluster_pages),
    'pillar': 1.5 if pillar_present else 0,
    'internal_density': internal_links / max(cluster_pages, 1),
    'external_authority': log(1 + external_authority),
    'ranking_share': ranking_share / len(topic_queries)
  })
入力
  • domain: str —
  • topic: str —
出力
  • score: float —

Adaptive Crawl Scheduling

Adaptive Crawl Scheduling crawling_indexing crawling 2002

$$\lambda(u, t) = \text{Poisson rate}, \quad t_{\text{next}}(u) = t_{\text{now}} + 1/\lambda(u)$$
Time O(|urls|) Space O(|urls|)
擬似コード
function adaptive_schedule(urls):
  for url in urls:
    last_change = url.last_modified
    freq = estimate_change_frequency(url.history)
    next_crawl = last_change + 1/freq
    schedule(url, next_crawl)

Best-First Focused Crawler

Best-First Focused crawling_indexing crawling 1999

Chakrabarti et al. (1999) WWW Focused Crawler。Topic classifier で優先度判定。

$$P_{\text{visit}}(u) = \frac{\text{topic-score}(u)}{\sum_v \text{topic-score}(v)}$$
Time O(|V| log |V|) Space O(|V|)
擬似コード
function best_first_crawl(seeds, target_topic):
  pq = PriorityQueue(seeds)
  while not pq.empty():
    url = pq.get()
    page = fetch(url)
    score = topic_similarity(page, target_topic)
    if score > threshold:
      yield page
    for link in extract_links(page):
      pq.put((-link_score(link, target_topic), link))

Bloom Filter for Crawl Frontier

Bloom Filter crawling_indexing indexing 1970

Bloom (1970) の確率データ構造。Crawler の URL 重複検出で標準。FP rate 1% で 9.6 bits/element。

$$P_{\text{FP}} = (1 - e^{-kn/m})^k, \quad \text{optimal } k = (m/n) \ln 2$$
Time O(k) per op Space O(m) bits
擬似コード
function bloom_insert(key, bit_array, hash_fns):
  for h in hash_fns:
    bit_array[h(key) % len(bit_array)] = 1

function bloom_contains(key, bit_array, hash_fns):
  return all(bit_array[h(key) % len(bit_array)] == 1 for h in hash_fns)

Breadth-First Crawler

BFS Crawler crawling_indexing crawling 1993

古典 BFS crawler。WWW Wanderer (1993)。crawler 設計の起源。

$$P_{\text{visit}}(u) = \text{FIFO order}, \quad \text{discover-distance}$$
Time O(|V| + |E|) Space O(|V|)
擬似コード
function bfs_crawl(seeds, max_pages):
  queue = deque(seeds)
  visited = set()
  while queue and len(visited) < max_pages:
    url = queue.popleft()
    if url in visited or not robots_allow(url): continue
    visited.add(url)
    page = fetch(url)
    for link in extract_links(page):
      if link not in visited:
        queue.append(link)

Cuckoo Filter

Cuckoo Filter crawling_indexing indexing 2014

Fan et al. (2014) の Bloom 後継。削除可能 + 高 FP 効率。Redis / TiDB 等で運用。

$$P_{\text{FP}} = 2b / 2^f, \quad b = \text{bucket size}, f = \text{fingerprint bits}$$
Time O(1) amortized Space O(n * f) bits
擬似コード
function cuckoo_insert(key, table):
  i1 = hash1(key) % table.size
  i2 = i1 XOR hash(fingerprint(key))
  if table[i1].empty: table[i1] = fingerprint(key)
  elif table[i2].empty: table[i2] = fingerprint(key)
  else: kick_and_relocate(i1, fingerprint(key), MAX_KICKS)

Distributed Crawler with Sharding

Distributed Crawler crawling_indexing crawling 1999

Mercator (Heydon-Najork 1999) の分散実装。Apache Nutch / Heritrix の基盤。

$$\text{shard}(u) = h(\text{host}(u)) \mod N$$
Time O(|V|/N + |E|/N) per shard Space O(|V|/N)
擬似コード
function distributed_crawl(seed_urls, n_shards=10):
  shards = [Worker(i) for i in range(n_shards)]
  for url in seed_urls:
    shard_id = hash(url.host) % n_shards
    shards[shard_id].queue(url)
  parallel_execute([shard.run() for shard in shards])

function fst_build(sorted_keys):
  # Lucene FST: shared suffix + outputs
  fst = FST()
  for key, value in sorted_keys:
    fst.add_with_output(key, value)
  return fst.minimize()  # state minimization

Incremental Crawler

function incremental_crawl(corpus, threshold=0.1):
  for doc in corpus:
    current = fetch(doc.url)
    if hash(current) != hash(doc.cached):
      doc.update(current)
      update_index(doc)
    else:
      doc.last_checked = now()

LSM-Tree Index

LSM-Tree crawling_indexing indexing 1996

O'Neil et al. (1996) Log-Structured Merge Tree。RocksDB / LevelDB / Cassandra の基盤。

$$\text{write-amp} = O(\log N / \log B), \quad \text{read-amp} = O(\log N)$$

擬似コード
function lsm_insert(key, value, memtable, sstables):
  memtable.put(key, value)
  if memtable.size > THRESHOLD:
    sstables.append(memtable.flush_to_disk())
    memtable.clear()
  if len(sstables) > MAX_LEVEL:
    compact(sstables)

Mercator Crawl Frontier

Mercator クロールフロンティア crawling_indexing crawling 1999

Heydon-Najork (1999) の分散クローラ。Googlebot / Bingbot / Apache Nutch の祖。Priority queue + politeness + duplicate detection。

$$P_{visit}(u) = \frac{\text{priority}(u)}{\sum_v \text{priority}(v)}, \quad \text{priority}(u) = \alpha \cdot \text{PR}(u) + \beta \cdot \text{freshness}(u)$$
Time O(|pages| * fetch_latency) Space O(|frontier| + |visited|)
擬似コード
function Mercator_crawl(seed_urls, max_pages):
  frontier = PriorityQueue()
  for url in seed_urls:
    frontier.push(url, priority=compute_priority(url))
  visited = set()
  while not frontier.empty() and len(visited) < max_pages:
    url = frontier.pop()
    if url in visited or not robots_allow(url): continue
    visited.add(url)
    page = fetch(url)
    for link in extract_links(page):
      if link not in visited:
        frontier.push(link, priority=compute_priority(link))
  return visited
入力
  • seeds: List[URL] —
  • robots_cache: Cache —
出力
  • crawled_pages: List[Page] —
Bench Mercator (Compaq SRC) — Foundational distributed crawler

function roaring_or(bm1, bm2):

Robots.txt Parser

Robots.txt Parser crawling_indexing crawling 2022

RFC 9309 (2022) 標準実装。Googlebot / Bingbot の official parser。

$$\text{Allow}(\text{UA}, p) = \text{longest-match-rule}(\text{UA}, p) = \text{Allow}$$
Time O(|robots.txt|) Space O(|rules|)
擬似コード
function parse_robots(robots_txt_content):
  rules = {}
  current_ua = '*'
  for line in robots_txt_content.split('\n'):
    if line.startswith('User-agent:'):
      current_ua = line.split(':')[1].strip()
    elif line.startswith('Disallow:'):
      path = line.split(':')[1].strip()
      rules.setdefault(current_ua, []).append(('disallow', path))
    elif line.startswith('Allow:'):
      path = line.split(':')[1].strip()
      rules.setdefault(current_ua, []).append(('allow', path))
  return rules
入力

出力
  • output: any — see description

function sse_search(encrypted_query, encrypted_index, sk):
  # Searchable Symmetric Encryption

  encrypted_results = encrypted_index.lookup(token)
  return decrypt(encrypted_results, sk)

Sitemap XML Generation

Sitemap XML 生成 crawling_indexing indexing 2008

sitemaps.org 0.9 protocol 準拠の生成。50K URL / 50MB 上限超は sitemap_index.xml で分割。

$$\text{priority}(u) \in [0.0, 1.0], \quad \text{changefreq} \in \{always, hourly, daily, weekly, monthly, yearly, never\}$$
Time O(|urls|) Space O(|urls|)
擬似コード
function generate_sitemap(urls, lastmod_map, output_path):
  with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
    f.write('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n')
    f.write('<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">\n')
    for url in urls:
      if not is_indexable(url): continue
      f.write('  <url>\n')
      f.write(f'    <loc>{xml_escape(url)}</loc>\n')
      f.write(f'    <lastmod>{lastmod_map[url]}</lastmod>\n')
      f.write(f'    <changefreq>{infer_changefreq(url)}</changefreq>\n')
      f.write(f'    <priority>{compute_priority(url):.1f}</priority>\n')
      f.write('  </url>\n')
    f.write('</urlset>\n')
  # If > 50K URLs, split into multiple sitemaps + sitemap_index.xml
入力
  • urls: List[URL] —
  • lastmod: Dict[URL, datetime] —
出力
  • sitemap_path: Path —

Trie Index for Autocomplete

Trie crawling_indexing indexing 1959

Fredkin (1959) の prefix tree。Google / Bing autocomplete の基礎構造。

$$\text{search}(p) = O(|p|) + O(|\text{results}|)$$
Time O(|p| + |results|) Space O(|vocabulary| * |alphabet|)
擬似コード
function trie_search(prefix, root):
  node = root
  for char in prefix:
    if char not in node.children:
      return []
    node = node.children[char]
  return [prefix + suffix for suffix in node.suffixes]

Cascade Click Model

カスケードクリックモデル evaluation_clickmodels evaluation 2008

Craswell et al. (2008) の cascade hypothesis。ユーザーは上から順に scan し、クリックしたら離脱。Position bias 推定の祖。

$$P(C_i=1 | R_{1..i}) = R_i \prod_{j<i} (1 - R_j)$$
Time O(k) per query Space O(k)
擬似コード
function cascade_likelihood(positions, clicks, relevance):
  # User scans top-down, clicks if attractive, then stops
  log_lik = 0
  for i in range(len(positions)):
    if clicks[i] == 1:
      log_lik += log(relevance[i])
      break  # user stops after click
    else:
      log_lik += log(1 - relevance[i])
  return log_lik
入力
  • click_sequence: List[0|1] —
出力
  • log_likelihood: float —
Bench WSDM 2008 — Foundational click model

Cohen's Kappa

Cohen's κ evaluation_clickmodels evaluation 1960

Cohen (1960) の評価者間一致係数。注意: 有病率パラドックスに弱い。本エコシステムでは Gwet AC1 を主指標、Kappa を参考値とする。

$$\kappa = \frac{P_o - P_e}{1 - P_e}$$
Time O(n) Space O(|C|)
擬似コード
function cohens_kappa(rater1, rater2):
  n = len(rater1)
  categories = set(rater1) | set(rater2)
  Po = sum(1 for a, b in zip(rater1, rater2) if a == b) / n
  Pe = 0
  for c in categories:
    p1 = rater1.count(c) / n
    p2 = rater2.count(c) / n
    Pe += p1 * p2
  return (Po - Pe) / (1 - Pe) if Pe != 1 else 1
入力
  • rater1: List —
  • rater2: List —
出力
  • kappa: float —
Bench Standard for IRR — Landis-Koch 1977 interpretation table

function dp_ips(clicks, epsilon=1.0):
  noisy_clicks = [c + laplace_noise(0, 1/epsilon) for c in clicks]

Gwet's AC1

Gwet AC1 evaluation_clickmodels evaluation 2008

Gwet (2008) の Kappa 代替。有病率パラドックスに頑健。本エコシステム全 DB の主品質指標。

$$\mathrm{AC1} = \frac{P_o - P_{e,\gamma}}{1 - P_{e,\gamma}}, \quad P_{e,\gamma} = \frac{1}{K-1}\sum_{c=1}^{K} p_c (1 - p_c)$$
Time O(n + K) Space O(K)
擬似コード
function gwet_ac1(rater1, rater2):
  n = len(rater1)
  categories = sorted(set(rater1) | set(rater2))
  K = len(categories)
  Po = sum(1 for a, b in zip(rater1, rater2) if a == b) / n
  marginals = {c: (rater1.count(c) + rater2.count(c)) / (2*n) for c in categories}
  Pe_gamma = sum(p * (1-p) for p in marginals.values()) / (K - 1)
  return (Po - Pe_gamma) / (1 - Pe_gamma)
入力
  • rater1: List —
  • rater2: List —
出力
  • ac1: float —
Bench SEO-DB Phase 13 pilot: 0.943 — 本エコシステム推奨主指標 (rule:quality-metrics-standard.md)
📄 参考実装/論文

Inverse Propensity Score (IPS) for LTR

IPS unbiased LTR evaluation_clickmodels learning 2017

Joachims et al. (2017) の inverse propensity scoring による counterfactual LTR。位置バイアスを補正。

$$\hat{\Delta}(f) = \frac{1}{|Q|}\sum_q \frac{1}{p(o=1|i,q)} \cdot \mathbb{I}[\mathrm{click}_q]$$
Time O(|Q| * k) Space O(|Q|)
擬似コード
function IPS_LTR_loss(clicks, positions, propensities, scores):
  # propensity p_i = probability user examined position i
  loss = 0
  for i in range(len(clicks)):
    if clicks[i] == 1:
      # Weight click by 1/propensity to debias
      weight = 1 / propensities[positions[i]]
      loss += weight * pairwise_loss(scores[i], scores[non_clicked])
  return loss
入力
  • click_logs: DataFrame —
  • propensities: Dict[position, float] —
出力
  • unbiased_estimator: float —
Bench Yahoo+propensity estimated — Standard in unbiased LTR

Local Differential Privacy for Clicks

LDP Click Logging evaluation_clickmodels evaluation 2016

Erlingsson RAPPOR の click logging への適用。Google Chrome で運用。

$$P(\text{reported} = \text{true}) = \frac{e^\epsilon}{e^\epsilon + 1}$$
Time O(1) per click Space O(1)
擬似コード
function ldp_click(true_click, epsilon=1.0):
  # Randomized Response
  p = exp(epsilon) / (exp(epsilon) + 1)
  if random() < p:
    return true_click
  else:
    return 1 - true_click  # flip

Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR)

MRR evaluation_clickmodels evaluation 1999

最初の関連文書の逆順位の平均。MS MARCO / TREC QA で標準。

$$\mathrm{MRR} = \frac{1}{|Q|} \sum_{q=1}^{|Q|} \frac{1}{\mathrm{rank}_q}$$
Time O(|Q| * k) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function MRR(rankings, relevant_ids):
  total = 0
  for query_idx in range(len(rankings)):
    for rank, doc_id in enumerate(rankings[query_idx], start=1):
      if doc_id in relevant_ids[query_idx]:
        total += 1 / rank
        break  # first relevant
  return total / len(rankings)
入力
  • rankings: List[List[int]] —
  • relevant: List[Set[int]] —
出力
  • mrr: float —
Bench MS MARCO MRR@10 — MS MARCO official metric

Multi-Armed Bandit (Thompson Sampling)

Thompson Sampling MAB evaluation_clickmodels learning 1933

Thompson (1933) のベイズ MAB。固定 A/B テストより少ないサンプルで勝者特定可能。SERP feature A/B、UI A/B、Snippet A/B で標準。

$$a_t \sim \arg\max_a \theta_a, \quad \theta_a \sim \mathrm{Beta}(\alpha_a, \beta_a)$$
Time O(|arms|) per round Space O(|arms|)
擬似コード
function thompson_sampling(arms, n_rounds):
  # Each arm has Beta(alpha, beta) belief over success rate
  alpha = [1] * len(arms)
  beta  = [1] * len(arms)
  history = []
  for t in range(n_rounds):
    # Sample success rate from each arm's posterior
    samples = [random.betavariate(alpha[a], beta[a]) for a in range(len(arms))]
    # Pull arm with highest sample
    chosen = argmax(samples)
    reward = arms[chosen].pull()  # 0 or 1
    # Update posterior
    if reward == 1: alpha[chosen] += 1
    else: beta[chosen] += 1
    history.append((chosen, reward))
  return history
入力
  • arms: List[Arm] —
  • n_rounds: int —
出力
  • trajectory: List[(arm, reward)] —
Bench Mortal MAB benchmarks — Bayesian regret bounded
📄 参考実装/論文

NDCG@k

NDCG@k evaluation_clickmodels evaluation 2002

Järvelin-Kekäläinen (2002) の Normalized DCG。多段階関連性ラベルで上位の順位の効果を強調。

$$\mathrm{NDCG@k} = \frac{1}{\mathrm{IDCG}@k} \sum_{i=1}^{k} \frac{2^{\mathrm{rel}_i} - 1}{\log_2(i+1)}$$
Time O(k log k) Space O(k)
擬似コード
function NDCG(ranking, relevance, k):
  DCG = sum(
    (2^relevance[ranking[i]] - 1) / log2(i + 2)
    for i in range(k)
  )
  ideal_ranking = sorted(range(len(relevance)), key=lambda i: -relevance[i])
  IDCG = sum(
    (2^relevance[ideal_ranking[i]] - 1) / log2(i + 2)
    for i in range(k)
  )
  return DCG / IDCG if IDCG > 0 else 0
入力
  • ranking: List[int] —
  • relevance: List[int] —
  • k: int —
出力
  • ndcg: float — 0..1, higher is better
Bench TREC ad-hoc, MS MARCO — Standard metric for graded relevance
📄 参考実装/論文

AI Overviews Source Selection

$$S_{AIO}(p) = 0.20 \cdot S_{schema}(p) + 0.25 \cdot S_{EEAT}(p) + 0.15 \cdot S_{struct}(p) + 0.15 \cdot S_{defclarity}(p) + 0.15 \cdot S_{auth}(p) + 0.10 \cdot S_{fresh}(p)$$

擬似コード
function select_aio_sources(query, candidates):
  scored = []
  for page in candidates:
    score = (
      schema_completeness(page) * 0.20 +
      eeat_composite(page, page.author, page.site) * 0.25 +
      structured_data_quality(page) * 0.15 +
      definition_clarity(page) * 0.15 +  # 短い明確な定義
      authority_signals(page.site) * 0.15 +
      freshness_score(page.modified) * 0.10
    )
    scored.append((page, score))
  return sorted(scored, key=lambda x: -x[1])[:5]

HyDE Zero-Shot Retrieval

HyDE generative_ai_search retrieval 2023

Gao et al. (2023) ACL の zero-shot dense retrieval。LLM 仮説文書を query proxy として埋め込む。

$$\hat{d} = \mathrm{LLM}(q), \mathrm{retrieve} = \mathrm{ANN}(\mathrm{enc}(\hat{d}))$$
Time O(LLM + ANN) Space O(corpus * dim)
擬似コード
function hyde_retrieve(query, llm, retriever):
  # 1. Generate hypothetical answer
  hypo_doc = llm.generate(f'Write a passage answering: {query}')
  # 2. Embed the hypothetical doc (proxy for query)
  h_hypo = retriever.encoder.encode(hypo_doc)
  # 3. Nearest real docs to hypothetical
  return retriever.faiss.search(h_hypo, k=10)

LLMO Score (LLM Optimization)

LLMO スコア generative_ai_search ranking 2024

GEO (Generative Engine Optimization) の核指標。複数 LLM での引用頻度 + Schema + 事実密度 + 定義強度。

$$\text{LLMO}(p) = 0.4 \cdot \frac{|\{l \in L : p \in \text{cite}_l(q_p)\}|}{|L|} + 0.2 \cdot S_{schema}(p) + 0.2 \cdot \rho_{fact}(p) + 0.2 \cdot S_{def}(p)$$
Time O(|llms| * query_latency) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function llmo_score(page, llm_clients):
  # Test: does the LLM cite this page when asked about its topic?
  query = page.main_topic
  citations = 0
  for llm in ['chatgpt', 'claude', 'gemini', 'perplexity']:
    response = llm_clients[llm].ask(query, browse=True)
    if page.url in response.cited_urls:
      citations += 1
  # Additional signals
  schema_score = structured_data_quality(page)
  fact_density = count_specific_facts(page.text) / page.word_count
  definition_strength = first_paragraph_definition_score(page)
  return citations * 0.4 + schema_score * 0.2 + fact_density * 0.2 + definition_strength * 0.2
入力
  • page: Page —
  • llm_clients: Dict[str,LLM] —
出力

function optimize_for_ai_citation(article):
  # Structural patterns that increase AI citation
  optimizations = []
  # 1. Definition at top
  if not has_definition_in_first_50_words(article):
    optimizations.append('add_definition_lead')
  # 2. Q&A format for FAQ-like queries
  if article.intent == 'informational':
    optimizations.append('add_qa_structure')
  # 3. Specific numbers + sources
  if facts_with_citations(article) < 5:
    optimizations.append('add_5_more_cited_facts')
  # 4. Schema.org Article + Person + Organization @graph
  if not has_complete_schema_graph(article):
    optimizations.append('add_schema_graph')
  # 5. Table for comparisons
  if has_comparison_intent(article) and not has_table(article):
    optimizations.append('convert_to_table')
  return optimizations

RAG with Citation

$$p(y | x) = \sum_{z \in \text{top-k}} p_\eta(z|x) \cdot p_\theta(y | x, z), \quad y = \text{generate}(\text{LLM}, [z_1, ..., z_k, x])$$

function rag_with_citation(query, retriever, llm):
  docs = retriever.search(query, k=5)
  context = '\n\n'.join(f'[{i+1}] {d.text}' for i, d in enumerate(docs))
  prompt = f'''Context:\n{context}\n\nQuestion: {query}\nAnswer (cite sources as [N]):'''
  answer = llm.generate(prompt)
  citations = extract_citation_numbers(answer)  # e.g. [1], [3]
  cited_sources = [docs[i-1] for i in citations]
  return answer, cited_sources

ReAct (Reasoning + Acting)

ReAct generative_ai_search learning 2022

Yao et al. (2022) の Reasoning + Acting 統合。HotpotQA / WebShop で SoTA。LangChain Agent の基盤。

$$\text{step}_t = (T_t, A_t, O_t), \quad A_t = \pi_\theta(T_t \mid \text{history}_{<t}), \quad O_t = \text{tool}(A_t)$$
Time O(steps * llm_call) Space O(scratchpad)
擬似コード
function react_agent(query, llm, tools):
  scratchpad = ''
  for step in range(max_steps):
    prompt = f'Question: {query}\n{scratchpad}\nThought: '
    thought = llm.generate(prompt, stop='Action:')
    action_prompt = prompt + thought + 'Action: '
    action = llm.generate(action_prompt, stop='Observation:')
    action_name, action_input = parse(action)
    if action_name == 'Finish': return action_input
    observation = tools[action_name].run(action_input)
    scratchpad += f'Thought: {thought}\nAction: {action}\nObservation: {observation}\n'
入力

  • tools: Dict[str,Tool] —
出力

Self-Consistency Decoding

Self-Consistency 推論 generative_ai_search learning 2022

Wang et al. (2022) ICLR の CoT サンプリング + majority vote。GenAI 検索の回答信頼性向上の標準。

$$\hat{y} = \arg\max_y \sum_{i=1}^{n} \mathbb{I}[y_i = y]$$
Time O(n * generate_latency) Space O(n)
擬似コード
function self_consistency_answer(query, llm, n_samples=20):
  # Generate multiple Chain-of-Thought paths
  samples = []
  for i in range(n_samples):
    prompt = "Let's think step by step.\n" + query
    cot = llm.generate(prompt, temperature=0.7)
    answer = extract_final_answer(cot)
    samples.append(answer)
  # Majority vote (or weighted vote by length / log prob)
  return mode(samples)
入力
  • query: str —
  • llm: LLM —
出力
  • answer: str —
Bench GSM8K, MATH — +17% absolute on GSM8K vs single CoT

Tree of Thoughts (ToT)

Tree of Thoughts generative_ai_search learning 2023

Yao et al. (2023) NeurIPS。Chain-of-Thought を木探索化。Game of 24 で GPT-4 4% → 74% へ改善。

$$\text{best-path}(\mathcal{T}) = \arg\max_{path} \sum_{n \in path} V(n), \quad \mathcal{T} = \text{beam-search}(b, d), V(n) \in \{\text{sure}, \text{maybe}, \text{impossible}\}$$
Time O(beam^depth * llm_call) Space O(beam^depth)
擬似コード
function tree_of_thoughts(problem, llm, max_depth=4, beam=5):
  root = Node(state=problem, score=0)
  frontier = [root]
  for depth in range(max_depth):
    candidates = []
    for node in frontier:
      thoughts = llm.generate_next_thoughts(node.state, k=beam)
      for thought in thoughts:
        new_state = node.state + thought
        score = llm.evaluate(new_state)  # 'sure'/'maybe'/'impossible'
        candidates.append(Node(state=new_state, score=score, parent=node))
    frontier = sorted(candidates, key=lambda n: -n.score)[:beam]
  return best_path(frontier[0])
入力
  • problem: str —
  • llm: LLM —
出力
  • solution_path: List[Thought] —
Bench —

2Q Cache

2Q ir_foundations indexing 1994

擬似コード
function two_q_get(key, A1in, A1out, Am, kin, kout):
  if key in Am:
    move_to_head_of(Am, key)  # hot
  elif key in A1in:
    pass  # in-recently
  elif key in A1out:
    move_to_Am(key)  # promote to hot
  else:
    add_to_A1in(key)

ARC (Adaptive Replacement Cache)

ARC ir_foundations indexing 2003

Megiddo-Modha (2003) の自己調整 cache。LRU + LFU の hybrid。IBM Storage で運用。

$$p = |T_1| / |c|, \quad \text{balance recency vs frequency}$$
Time O(1) per op Space O(c)
擬似コード
function arc_get(key, T1, T2, B1, B2, p):
  if key in T1 or T2:
    move_to_T2(key)  # recency → frequency
  elif key in B1:
    p = min(p + delta1, c)
    move_to_T2(key)
  elif key in B2:
    p = max(p - delta2, 0)
    move_to_T2(key)
  return value

BM25

BM25 (Okapi BM25) ir_foundations ranking 1994

Probabilistic relevance framework に基づく古典スコア関数。Lucene/Elasticsearch/Solr の事実上の標準。k1=1.2, b=0.75 が最も広く使用。

$$\mathrm{BM25}(q,d) = \sum_{t \in q} \mathrm{IDF}(t) \cdot \frac{f(t,d)\cdot(k_1+1)}{f(t,d) + k_1\cdot\bigl(1 - b + b\cdot \frac{|d|}{\overline{|d|}}\bigr)}$$
Time O(|q| * avg_postings) Space O(|V|)
擬似コード
function BM25(query, doc, corpus):
  k1 = 1.2  # term frequency saturation
  b  = 0.75 # length normalization
  avgdl = mean([len(d) for d in corpus])
  score = 0
  for term in query:
    f = count(term, doc)
    if f == 0: continue
    idf = log((N - n_t + 0.5) / (n_t + 0.5) + 1)
    norm = 1 - b + b * (len(doc) / avgdl)
    score += idf * (f * (k1 + 1)) / (f + k1 * norm)
  return score
入力
  • query: List[str] — tokenized query terms
  • doc: Document — candidate document with term freqs and length
  • corpus_stats: Dict — N (total docs), n_t (docs containing t), avgdl
出力
  • score: float — BM25 relevance score (higher = more relevant)
Bench MS MARCO Passage Ranking — MRR@10 ≈ 0.187 on MS MARCO passage (baseline)
📄 参考実装/論文

Belady's Optimal

Belady's MIN ir_foundations indexing 1966

Belady (1966) の theoretical optimal。実装不可能だが lower bound 比較に使用。

$$\text{evict}^* = \arg\max_x \text{next-use-distance}(x)$$
Time O(n^2) Space O(n)
擬似コード
function belady_optimal(reference_sequence, cache_size):
  cache = set()
  hits = 0
  for i, page in enumerate(reference_sequence):
    if page in cache:
      hits += 1
    else:
      if len(cache) >= cache_size:
        # Evict page that will be used farthest in future
        evict = max(cache, key=lambda p: future_use(p, reference_sequence[i:]))
        cache.remove(evict)
      cache.add(page)
  return hits
入力

出力
  • output: any — see description
Bench Theoretical optimal — Upper bound

LRU Cache

LRU Cache ir_foundations indexing 1965

Belady (1965) の baseline cache replacement。Page replacement の古典。

$$\text{evict}(c) = \arg\min_{x \in c} \text{last-used}(x)$$
Time O(1) per op Space O(max_size)
擬似コード
function lru_get(key, cache, max_size):
  if key in cache:
    cache.move_to_end(key)  # mark as recent
    return cache[key]
  return None

function lru_put(key, value, cache, max_size):
  cache[key] = value
  cache.move_to_end(key)
  if len(cache) > max_size:
    cache.popitem(last=False)  # evict oldest

Locality Sensitive Hashing (LSH)

LSH ir_foundations indexing 1998

Indyk-Motwani (1998) のサブリニア NN 探索。20 bands × 5 rows でJaccard 0.8 候補を高再現率で抽出。

$$P(\mathrm{collision}) = 1 - (1 - s^r)^b$$
Time O(|docs| * bands) Space O(|docs| * bands)
擬似コード
function build_lsh_index(signatures, bands=20, rows=5):
  # signatures: each is num_hashes long, num_hashes = bands * rows
  buckets = defaultdict(set)
  for doc_id, sig in signatures.items():
    for b in range(bands):
      band = tuple(sig[b*rows:(b+1)*rows])
      buckets[(b, band)].add(doc_id)
  return buckets

function find_candidates(query_sig, buckets, bands=20, rows=5):
  candidates = set()
  for b in range(bands):
    band = tuple(query_sig[b*rows:(b+1)*rows])
    candidates |= buckets.get((b, band), set())
  return candidates
入力
  • signatures: Dict —
  • bands: int —
  • rows: int —
出力
  • index: Dict —
Bench —

MinHash Shingling

MinHash シングル化 ir_foundations indexing 1997

Broder (1997) AltaVista の near-duplicate 検出。シグネチャ長 128 で Jaccard 推定誤差 ±9%。

$$\hat{J}(A,B) = \frac{1}{k} \sum_{i=1}^k \mathbb{I}[h_i(A) = h_i(B)]$$
Time O(|text| * num_hashes) Space O(num_hashes)
擬似コード
function minhash_signature(text, k=5, num_hashes=128):
  shingles = set()
  for i in range(len(text) - k + 1):
    shingles.add(text[i:i+k])
  # Apply random hash functions
  hashes = [random_hash() for _ in range(num_hashes)]
  signature = []
  for h in hashes:
    min_val = min(h(s) for s in shingles)
    signature.append(min_val)
  return signature

function jaccard_estimate(sig1, sig2):
  return sum(s1 == s2 for s1, s2 in zip(sig1, sig2)) / len(sig1)
入力
  • text: str —
出力
  • signature: List[int] —
Bench —

Simhash for Duplicate Detection

Simhash ir_foundations indexing 2002

Manku-Jain-Sarma (2002) Google の near-duplicate 検出。Hamming distance ≤ 3 で重複判定。Web crawl で標準。

$$d_H(s_1, s_2) = \mathrm{popcount}(s_1 \oplus s_2)$$
Time O(|tokens|) Space O(num_bits)
擬似コード
function simhash(text, num_bits=64):
  v = [0] * num_bits
  for token in tokenize(text):
    h = hash(token) & ((1 << num_bits) - 1)
    weight = tf_idf(token)
    for i in range(num_bits):
      v[i] += weight if (h >> i) & 1 else -weight
  return sum((1 << i) for i in range(num_bits) if v[i] > 0)

function similar(s1, s2, threshold=3):
  return bin(s1 ^ s2).count('1') <= threshold  # Hamming distance
入力
  • text: str —
出力
  • hash: int —
Bench —

Skip List

function skiplist_search(key, head):
  node = head
  for level in range(MAX_LEVEL, -1, -1):
    while node.forward[level] and node.forward[level].key < key:
      node = node.forward[level]
  node = node.forward[0]
  return node if node.key == key else None

TF-IDF

TF-IDF ir_foundations ranking 1972

Term Frequency × Inverse Document Frequency の古典。BM25 の前駆。VSM の中核。

$$\mathrm{tfidf}(t,d,D) = \mathrm{tf}(t,d) \cdot \log \frac{|D|}{1 + |\{d' \in D : t \in d'\}|}$$
Time O(|q| * |doc|) Space O(|V|)
擬似コード
function TF_IDF(query, doc, corpus):
  score = 0
  for term in query:
    tf  = count(term, doc) / len(doc)
    idf = log(N / (1 + n_t))  # smoothed
    score += tf * idf
  return score
入力
  • query: List[str] — query terms
  • doc: Document — candidate doc
出力
  • score: float — TF-IDF score
Bench TREC ad-hoc — Historical baseline

function approx_ndcg_loss(s, y):
  # Soft sorting via Plackett-Luce permutation prob
  P = softmax_temp(s, T=tau)
  return -sum(y_i / log2(1 + i_smooth(P)) for i in ranks)

Borda Count

Borda Count Voting learning_to_rank ranking 1781

Borda 投票制度 (1781)。IR 融合で広く使用。Condorcet 系の正統性。

$$\text{Borda}(d) = \sum_r (n - \text{rank}_r(d))$$
Time O(|R| * |docs|) Space O(|docs|)
擬似コード
function borda_count(rankings):
  scores = defaultdict(int)
  n = len(rankings[0])
  for ranking in rankings:
    for rank, doc in enumerate(ranking):
      scores[doc] += n - rank
  return sorted(scores.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])
入力

Calibrated Score Fusion

Calibrated Fusion learning_to_rank ranking 2021

Lin et al. (2021) のスコア正規化融合。RRF より調整可能性高い。

$$s_{\text{cal}} = \alpha \cdot \text{norm}(s_{\text{BM25}}) + (1-\alpha) \cdot \text{norm}(s_{\text{dense}})$$
Time O(|docs|) Space O(|docs|)
擬似コード
function calibrated_fusion(bm25_scores, dense_scores, alpha=0.5):
  # Normalize scores via min-max or z-score
  bm25_norm = (bm25_scores - bm25_scores.min()) / (bm25_scores.max() - bm25_scores.min())
  dense_norm = (dense_scores - dense_scores.mean()) / dense_scores.std()
  return alpha * bm25_norm + (1 - alpha) * dense_norm

CombMNZ Fusion

CombMNZ learning_to_rank ranking 1994

Fox-Shaw (1994) の頻度乗算融合。複数 ranker で同じ doc が出るほど boost。

$$\text{CombMNZ}(d) = (\sum_r s_r(d)) \cdot |\{r : d \in r\}|$$
Time O(|R| * |docs|) Space O(|docs|)
擬似コード
function combmnz(rankings):
  scores = defaultdict(lambda: [0, 0])  # [sum, count]
  for ranking in rankings:
    for doc, score in ranking:
      scores[doc][0] += score
      scores[doc][1] += 1
  return sorted([(d, s[0]*s[1]) for d, s in scores.items()], key=lambda x: -x[1])

function combsum(rankings):
  scores = defaultdict(float)
  for ranking in rankings:
    for doc, score in ranking:
      scores[doc] += score  # raw score sum
  return sorted(scores.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])

Distillation-based Reranker

Distilled Cross-Encoder learning_to_rank ranking 2020

擬似コード
function distill_train(teacher, student, queries):
  for q, p in queries:
    teacher_score = teacher(q, p).detach()
    student_score = student(q, p)
    loss = MSE(student_score, teacher_score)
    optimizer.step(loss)

FRank (Fidelity Rank)

FRank learning_to_rank learning 2007

Tsai et al. (2007) Microsoft の Fidelity loss。RankNet の代替。

$$\mathcal{L}_{\text{Fidelity}} = 1 - \sqrt{P_{\text{pred}} P_{\text{true}}} - \sqrt{(1-P_{\text{pred}})(1-P_{\text{true}})}$$
Time O(n^2) Space O(n)
擬似コード
function frank_loss(s, y):
  # Fidelity loss instead of cross-entropy
  p_pred = sigmoid(s_i - s_j)
  p_true = (1 + sign(y_i - y_j)) / 2
  fidelity = sqrt(p_pred * p_true) + sqrt((1-p_pred) * (1-p_true))
  return 1 - fidelity
入力

Federated Learning to Rank

Federated LTR learning_to_rank learning 2020

FedAvg を LTR に応用。プライバシー保護で各 client が local rank model 学習。

$$\theta_{t+1} = \theta_t + \eta \cdot \frac{1}{K} \sum_k (\theta_k - \theta_t)$$
Time O(K * local_step) Space O(K * model_params)
擬似コード
function federated_ltr(clients, rounds=100):
  global_model = init_model()
  for r in range(rounds):
    client_updates = []
    for client in clients:
      local_model = global_model.copy()
      local_model.fine_tune(client.private_data)
      client_updates.append(local_model.weights - global_model.weights)
    # FedAvg
    global_model.weights += mean(client_updates)
  return global_model
入力

出力
  • output: any — see description
Bench Synthetic Yandex — 0.421

Graph-based Diversity Reranking

Graph Diversity Rerank learning_to_rank ranking 2006

Carbonell-Goldstein MMR の graph 版。SERP 多様化の標準。

$$d^* = \arg\max_d [\text{rel}(d) - \lambda \cdot \max_{s \in S} \text{sim}(d, s)]$$
Time O(k * |top_k|^2) Space O(|top_k|^2)
擬似コード
function graph_diversify(top_k, similarity_graph, lambda_div=0.5):
  selected = [top_k[0]]
  for _ in range(k - 1):
    candidate = max(top_k - selected,
                    key=lambda d: rel(d) - lambda_div * max(sim(d, s) for s in selected))
    selected.append(candidate)
  return selected

function learned_hybrid(q, splade_scores, dense_scores, learned_weights):

LambdaLoss Framework

LambdaLoss learning_to_rank learning 2018

Wang et al. (2018) CIKM の LambdaRank 一般化。NDCG/ERR/MAP を直接最適化。Google が CIKM 2018 で発表。

$$\mathcal{L}_{\lambda} = \sum_{(i,j)} |\Delta M_{ij}| \log(1 + e^{-\sigma(s_i - s_j)})$$
Time O(|pairs| * k) Space O(|list|)
擬似コード
function lambdaloss_ndcg(scores, relevance, k=10):
  # Generalization of LambdaRank with explicit IR metric optimization
  loss = 0
  ranking = argsort(scores, desc=True)
  for i, j in pairs(range(len(scores))):
    if relevance[i] == relevance[j]: continue
    delta_metric = abs(ndcg_at_k(swap(ranking, i, j), relevance, k) - ndcg_at_k(ranking, relevance, k))
    sigma = 1
    loss += log(1 + exp(-sigma * (scores[i] - scores[j]))) * delta_metric
  return loss
入力
  • scores: List —
  • relevance: List —
出力
  • loss: float —
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

LambdaMART

LambdaMART learning_to_rank learning 2010

Burges (2010) の MART + LambdaRank 統合。Yahoo LTR Challenge 2010 で優勝。多くの商用検索 LTR の標準ベースライン。

$$\lambda_i = \sum_{j: (i,j)\in I} \frac{-\sigma}{1 + e^{\sigma(s_i - s_j)}} \cdot |\Delta \mathrm{NDCG}_{ij}|$$
Time O(T * N * F) T=trees, N=docs, F=features Space O(T * tree_size)
擬似コード
function LambdaMART(queries, max_trees=500, lr=0.1):
  # MART = Multiple Additive Regression Trees
  F = constant_model(0)
  for t in 1..max_trees:
    lambdas, weights = compute_lambdas(F, queries)
    # lambda_i = sum_j (delta_NDCG_ij) * sigma(s_j - s_i)
    tree = fit_regression_tree(features, target=lambdas, weights=weights)
    F = F + lr * tree
  return F
入力
  • training_queries: List[Query] — queries with graded relevance labels
出力
  • ensemble_model: TreeEnsemble — additive regression tree ensemble
Bench Yahoo LTR Set 1: ERR=0.466 — Yahoo LTR Challenge 2010 winner
📄 参考実装/論文

ListNet Loss

ListNet learning_to_rank learning 2007

Cao et al. (2007) ICML の listwise LTR。Plackett-Luce による順列確率 + cross-entropy。

$$\mathcal{L} = -\sum_i P_y(i) \log P_s(i), \quad P_s(i) = \mathrm{softmax}(s_i)$$
Time O(|list|) Space O(|list|)
擬似コード
function listnet_loss(scores, relevance):
  # Plackett-Luce: probability of permutation given scores
  P_y = softmax(relevance)  # ground truth distribution
  P_s = softmax(scores)     # predicted distribution
  # Cross-entropy
  return -sum(P_y[i] * log(P_s[i]) for i in range(len(scores)))
入力
  • scores: List[float] —
  • relevance: List[float] —
出力
  • loss: float —
Bench —

function listt5_rerank(query, passages):

  encoded = [t5.encode(f"Q:{query} P:{p}") for p in passages]
  decoder_input = concat(encoded)

Outranking Approach

Outranking learning_to_rank ranking 2008

Pairwise majority voting (MCDA outranking 由来)。Condorcet 一貫性。

$$d_1 > d_2 \Leftrightarrow \frac{|\{r : \text{rank}_r(d_1) < \text{rank}_r(d_2)\}|}{|R|} > \theta$$
Time O(|docs|^2 * |R|) Space O(|docs|^2)
擬似コード
function outranking(rankings, threshold=0.5):
  # Pairwise comparison voting
  beats = defaultdict(int)
  for d1 in docs:
    for d2 in docs:
      if d1 != d2:
        votes = sum(1 for r in rankings if r.rank(d1) < r.rank(d2))
        if votes / len(rankings) > threshold:
          beats[d1] += 1
  return sorted(docs, key=lambda d: -beats[d])
入力
  • input: any — see description
出力
  • output: any — see description
Bench N/A — N/A

function prop_pretrain(corpus):

Probabilistic Score Fusion

Probabilistic Fusion learning_to_rank ranking 2011

Bayesian framework の score fusion。事前分布で各 ranker の信頼性反映。

$$P(\text{relevant} | s_1, ..., s_n) \propto \prod_i P(s_i | r_i) \cdot P(\text{relevant})$$
Time O(|R| * |docs|) Space O(|docs|)
擬似コード
function prob_fusion(scores_list, priors):
  # Bayesian score combination
  posteriors = []
  for d in docs:
    likelihood = product(p(s_d | r) for s_d, r in zip(scores_d_list, rankers))
    posteriors.append((d, likelihood * priors[d]))
  return sorted(posteriors, key=lambda x: -x[1])

RRF (Reciprocal Rank Fusion)

RRF learning_to_rank ranking 2009

Cormack-Clarke-Büttcher (2009) SIGIR の rank fusion。Sparse + Dense ハイブリッド検索の事実上の標準融合手法。

$$\mathrm{RRF}(d) = \sum_{r \in R} \frac{1}{k + r(d)}$$
Time O(|R| * |docs|) Space O(|docs|)
擬似コード
function RRF(ranking_lists, k=60):
  # k=60 is empirically optimal (Cormack 2009)
  scores = defaultdict(float)
  for ranking in ranking_lists:
    for rank, doc_id in enumerate(ranking, start=1):
      scores[doc_id] += 1 / (k + rank)
  return sorted(scores.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])
入力
  • ranking_lists: List[List[doc_id]] —
  • k: int — default 60
出力
  • fused_ranking: List[(doc_id, score)] —
Bench TREC 9-11 — Hybrid Search 業界標準

RankNet

RankNet learning_to_rank learning 2005

Burges et al. (2005) Microsoft の neural pairwise LTR。LambdaRank/LambdaMART の前駆。Bing のランキングシステムで2010年代に運用。

$$\mathcal{L}(s_i, s_j) = -\bar{P}_{ij} \log(P_{ij}) - (1-\bar{P}_{ij}) \log(1-P_{ij}), \quad P_{ij} = \sigma(s_i - s_j)$$
Time O(P) per epoch, P = #pairs Space O(model_params)
擬似コード
function RankNet_loss(s_i, s_j, y_ij):
  # y_ij = 1 if i preferred over j, 0 otherwise
  P_ij = 1 / (1 + exp(-(s_i - s_j)))  # logistic
  loss = -y_ij * log(P_ij) - (1 - y_ij) * log(1 - P_ij)
  return loss

# Training: backprop through neural network producing s_i = f(x_i)
入力
  • pair (x_i, x_j, y_ij): Tuple — feature vectors + preference label
出力
  • loss: float — pairwise cross-entropy
Bench LETOR 4.0 — Bing 初期 LTR の中核
📄 参考実装/論文

Online learning to rank with click feedback。Etsy/Wayfair 等で実運用。

$$\theta_{t+1} = \theta_t - \eta_t \nabla_\theta \mathcal{L}_{\text{pair}}(x_+, x_-)$$
Time O(d) per update Space O(model_params)
擬似コード
function online_ranknet(user_clicks):
  for click_event in user_clicks:
    pos_doc = click_event.clicked
    neg_doc = click_event.skipped
    grad = backprop_pairwise(pos_doc, neg_doc)
    optimizer.update(grad)

Reranker Cascade with Budget

Cascade Rerank w/ Budget learning_to_rank ranking 2020

Multi-stage retrieval with budget constraints。各 stage で expensive scorer。

$$\text{Cascade}: C_0 \xrightarrow{\text{BM25}} C_1 \xrightarrow{\text{BiEnc}} C_2 \xrightarrow{\text{CrossEnc}} C_3$$
Time O(|C_0| + ... + |C_2| * L^2 d) Space O(|C_0|)
擬似コード
function cascade_rerank(q, candidates, budgets=[1000, 100, 10]):
  # Multi-stage with shrinking candidate sets
  curr = candidates
  for stage, budget in enumerate(budgets):
    if stage == 0: scorer = bm25
    elif stage == 1: scorer = bi_encoder
    else: scorer = cross_encoder
    curr = top_k(scorer.score(q, curr), k=budget)
  return curr

Self-Rerank with LLM

Self-Rerank learning_to_rank ranking 2023

LLM-as-Judge で pointwise rerank。Zero-shot で BM25 大幅改善。

$$s_i = \text{LLM-judge}(q, p_i) \in [0, 10]$$
Time O(LLM per pair) Space O(LLM)
擬似コード
function self_rerank(query, passages, llm):
  # LLM self-evaluates and reranks
  scored = []
  for p in passages:
    score = llm.judge(f"How relevant is {p} to {query}? Score 0-10")
    scored.append((p, parse_score(score)))
  return sorted(scored, key=lambda x: -x[1])

出力
  • output: any — see description
Bench BEIR avg — 0.501

Setwise Ranker

Setwise Ranker learning_to_rank ranking 2024

Zhuang et al. (2024) Google の setwise LLM reranker。Pairwise より効率、listwise より低コスト。

$$\text{best}_S = \arg\max_{p \in S} \text{LLM}(q, S)$$
Time O(|P|/k * LLM call) Space O(LLM)
擬似コード
function setwise_rerank(query, passages, k=5):
  # Process k-passage sets, more efficient than listwise
  results = []
  for batch in chunks(passages, k):
    prompt = f"Best of: {batch}? for query: {query}"
    best = llm.generate(prompt)
    results.append(best)
  return results

Tournament Ranking

Tournament Ranking learning_to_rank ranking 2024

Tournament-style pairwise LLM ranking。Listwise の長 context 問題回避。

$$O(n \log n) \text{ comparisons}$$
Time O(n log n) LLM calls Space O(n)
擬似コード
function tournament_rank(query, passages, llm):
  # k-way single elimination
  current = list(passages)
  while len(current) > 1:
    next_round = []
    for i in range(0, len(current), 2):
      winner = llm.compare(query, current[i], current[i+1])
      next_round.append(winner)
    current = next_round
  return current[0]  # winner of tournament
入力
  • input: any — see description
出力
  • output: any — see description
Bench MS MARCO — 0.382

Weighted RRF

α-Weighted RRF learning_to_rank ranking 2009

RRF の重み付き版。BM25:0.5 + Dense:0.5 の典型構成。

$$\text{wRRF}(d) = \sum_{r \in R} \frac{w_r}{k + r(d)}, \quad \sum w_r = 1$$
Time O(|R| * |docs|) Space O(|docs|)
擬似コード
function weighted_rrf(rankings, weights, k=60):
  scores = defaultdict(float)
  for ranking, w in zip(rankings, weights):
    for rank, doc in enumerate(ranking, 1):
      scores[doc] += w / (k + rank)
  return sorted(scores.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])

Anchor Text Distribution Audit

アンカーテキスト分布監査 link_analysis evaluation 2012

Penguin 検出回避のため exact-match anchor < 5%、brand > 30% を目安。月次監査で over-optimization 早期発見。

$$D(d) = \{r_{exact}, r_{partial}, r_{brand}, r_{naked}, r_{generic}, r_{image}\}, \quad \sum r_i = 1, \quad \text{warning if } r_{exact} > 0.05 \lor r_{brand} < 0.30$$
Time O(|backlinks|) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function audit_anchor_distribution(domain):
  backlinks = fetch_backlinks(domain)
  total = len(backlinks)
  distribution = {
    'exact_match': count(b for b in backlinks if b.anchor in domain.target_kws) / total,
    'partial_match': count(b for b in backlinks if any(kw in b.anchor for kw in domain.target_kws)) / total,
    'brand': count(b for b in backlinks if domain.brand_name in b.anchor) / total,
    'naked_url': count(b for b in backlinks if b.anchor.startswith('http')) / total,
    'generic': count(b for b in backlinks if b.anchor in ['click here', 'read more', 'こちら']) / total,
    'image': count(b for b in backlinks if b.is_image_link) / total
  }
  # Healthy: brand 40-60%, exact <5%, generic 10-20%
  warnings = []
  if distribution['exact_match'] > 0.05: warnings.append('over_optimized')
  if distribution['brand'] < 0.30: warnings.append('low_brand_signal')
  return distribution, warnings
入力
  • domain: str —
出力
  • distribution: Dict —
  • warnings: List[str] —
Bench —

function citation_velocity(paper, time_window=2):
  recent_citations = [c for c in paper.citations if (now() - c.date) < time_window * 365]
  return len(recent_citations) / time_window

Disavow File Generator

Disavow ファイル生成 link_analysis query_processing 2012

Ahrefs DR < 10 または Spam Score > 70 のリンクを抽出して disavow.txt 生成。GSC から送信。

$$\text{disavow}(d) = \{b : \text{DR}(b.\text{source}) < 10 \lor S_{spam}(b.\text{source}) > 70\}, \quad \text{by-domain if } |\text{links}| > 5$$
Time O(|backlinks|) Space O(|toxic|)
擬似コード
function generate_disavow(domain, threshold=10):
  backlinks = ahrefs_api.fetch_backlinks(domain)
  toxic = [b for b in backlinks if b.domain_rating < threshold or b.spam_score > 70]
  # Group by domain for efficiency
  by_domain = defaultdict(list)
  for b in toxic: by_domain[b.source_domain].append(b)
  lines = ['# Disavow file generated ' + datetime.now().isoformat()]
  for d, links in by_domain.items():
    if len(links) > 5:
      lines.append(f'domain:{d}')  # whole domain
    else:
      lines.extend(b.source_url for b in links)
  return '\n'.join(lines)

出力

HITS (Hyperlink-Induced Topic Search)

HITS link_analysis ranking 1999

Kleinberg (1999) のハブ-権威分離アルゴリズム。クエリ依存の topical subgraph に適用。Eigenvector centrality の応用。

$$a^{(k+1)}_v = \sum_{u \in \mathrm{In}(v)} h^{(k)}_u, \quad h^{(k+1)}_v = \sum_{u \in \mathrm{Out}(v)} a^{(k)}_u$$
Time O(k * (|V| + |E|)) Space O(|V|)
擬似コード
function HITS(graph, max_iter=100):
  h = {v: 1 for v in graph.nodes}  # hub scores
  a = {v: 1 for v in graph.nodes}  # authority scores
  for iter in range(max_iter):
    for v in graph.nodes:
      a[v] = sum(h[u] for u in graph.in_edges(v))
    for v in graph.nodes:
      h[v] = sum(a[u] for u in graph.out_edges(v))
    normalize(a); normalize(h)
  return h, a
入力
  • graph: DirectedGraph — topical subgraph
出力
  • hubs: Dict[node, float] — hub scores
  • authorities: Dict[node, float] — authority scores
Bench WT10g — Used in Teoma → Ask.com
📄 参考実装/論文

HITS Topical Subgraph

HITS on Topical Subgraph link_analysis ranking 1999

Kleinberg HITS の topical subgraph 適用。Teoma → Ask.com で運用。

$$\mathbf{a}^{(k+1)} = A^\top \mathbf{h}^{(k)}, \quad \mathbf{h}^{(k+1)} = A \mathbf{a}^{(k)}$$
Time O(k|V_{base}|^2) Space O(|V_{base}|^2)
擬似コード
function hits_topical(query, web_graph, base_set_size=200):
  # 1. Root set from search engine
  root = search(query, k=base_set_size)
  # 2. Base set: root + 1-step neighbors
  base = root + [n for r in root for n in web_graph.neighbors(r)]
  # 3. HITS on subgraph
  return hits_iteration(base)

Link Reclamation

リンク回収 link_analysis query_processing 2014

未リンク言及 + 壊れたバックリンク + 失われたバックリンクの3経路を統合。Ahrefs Lost Backlinks Report で月次運用。

$$\text{candidates}(d) = \{m : \text{brand} \in m \land d \notin \text{outlinks}(m)\} \cup \{b : \text{HTTP}(b.\text{target}) = 404\} \cup \text{lost-backlinks}_{90d}$$
Time O(|mentions| + |backlinks|) Space O(|candidates|)
擬似コード
function find_reclaimable_links(domain):
  candidates = []
  # 1. Unlinked brand mentions
  mentions = google_search(f'\"{domain.brand}\" -site:{domain}')
  for m in mentions:
    if domain not in m.outbound_links:
      candidates.append({'type': 'unlinked_mention', 'url': m.url})
  # 2. Broken backlinks (404 destination)
  for bl in fetch_backlinks(domain):
    if fetch(bl.target).status == 404:
      # Suggest redirect or update
      candidates.append({'type': 'broken_destination', 'source': bl.source, 'target': bl.target})
  # 3. Lost backlinks (recently removed)
  lost = ahrefs_api.lost_backlinks(domain, last_n_days=90)
  candidates.extend([{'type': 'lost', 'url': bl.source} for bl in lost])
  return candidates
入力

出力

Link Velocity Anomaly Detection

リンク速度異常検出 link_analysis evaluation 2014

Penguin の自動検出を未然に防ぐため、リンク速度の z-score > 3 を異常として監視。Ahrefs / SEMrush で時系列観測。

$$z = \frac{\bar{x}_{\text{recent}} - \bar{x}_{\text{baseline}}}{\sigma_{\text{baseline}}}$$
Time O(|days|) Space O(|days|)
擬似コード
function detect_velocity_anomaly(domain, window=30):
  daily = backlinks_per_day(domain, last_n_days=365)
  recent = daily[-window:]
  baseline = daily[-365:-window]
  recent_mean = mean(recent)
  baseline_mean = mean(baseline)
  baseline_std = std(baseline)
  z_score = (recent_mean - baseline_mean) / (baseline_std + 1)
  return {
    'recent_mean': recent_mean,
    'baseline_mean': baseline_mean,
    'z_score': z_score,
    'anomaly': abs(z_score) > 3,
    'direction': 'spike' if z_score > 3 else 'drop' if z_score < -3 else 'normal'
  }
入力
  • domain: str —
出力
  • report: Dict —
Bench —

PageRank Power Iteration

PageRank 反復計算 link_analysis ranking 1998

Brin-Page (1998) の Google 初期アルゴリズム。Power iteration による反復計算。d=0.85 が標準。Stochastic matrix の主固有ベクトル計算と等価。

$$PR(v) = \frac{1-d}{N} + d \cdot \sum_{u \in \mathrm{In}(v)} \frac{PR(u)}{|\mathrm{Out}(u)|}$$
Time O(k * (|V| + |E|)) k=iterations Space O(|V| + |E|)
擬似コード
function PageRank(graph, d=0.85, max_iter=100, eps=1e-8):
  N = |graph.nodes|
  PR = {v: 1/N for v in graph.nodes}
  for iter in range(max_iter):
    PR_new = {v: (1-d)/N for v in graph.nodes}
    for v in graph.nodes:
      for u in graph.in_edges(v):
        PR_new[v] += d * PR[u] / |graph.out_edges(u)|
    if max(|PR_new[v] - PR[v]|) < eps: break
    PR = PR_new
  return PR
入力
  • graph: DirectedGraph — web link graph
  • d: float — damping factor (typically 0.85)
出力
  • PR: Dict[node, float] — PageRank values
Bench Stanford WebGraph — Converges in ~50 iterations for d=0.85
📄 参考実装/論文

function ppr_retrieve(query, graph, alpha=0.85):

Topical PageRank

Topic-Sensitive PageRank link_analysis ranking 2002

Haveliwala (2002) のトピック特化 PageRank。teleport vector を topic seed pages に bias。Personalized Search の基盤。

$$PR_t(v) = (1-d) \cdot s_t(v) + d \sum_{u \to v} \frac{PR_t(u)}{|\mathrm{Out}(u)|}$$
Time O(k * (|V| + |E|)) Space O(|V|)
擬似コード
function topic_sensitive_pagerank(graph, topic, topic_pages, d=0.85):
  N = len(graph.nodes)
  # Bias teleport vector to topic pages
  pr = {v: 1/N for v in graph.nodes}
  for _ in range(100):
    pr_new = {}
    for v in graph.nodes:
      teleport = 1/len(topic_pages) if v in topic_pages else 0
      link_sum = sum(pr[u] / out_degree(u) for u in graph.in_edges(v))
      pr_new[v] = (1-d) * teleport + d * link_sum
    pr = pr_new
  return pr
入力
  • graph: Graph —
  • topic_pages: Set —
出力
  • pr: Dict —
Bench —

Beta-Binomial Bayesian Product Rating

Beta-Binomial ベイズ商品評価 local_vertical_multimodal ranking 2009

Miller (2009) のレビュー smoothing 手法。1件★5 が 100件★4.9 を上回らないようベイズ事前で smoothing。Local SEO の AggregateRating 評価で活用。

$$\hat{p}_{\mathrm{LB}} = \frac{\alpha_0 + k}{\alpha_0 + \beta_0 + n} - z_{0.95} \sqrt{\frac{(\alpha_0+k)(\beta_0+n-k)}{(\alpha_0+\beta_0+n)^2 (\alpha_0+\beta_0+n+1)}}$$
Time O(1) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function bayesian_rating(positive, total, alpha_prior=10, beta_prior=10):
  # Wilson / Beta-Binomial smoothing for sparse reviews
  # Avoids: '1 review with 5 stars' beating '100 reviews with 4.9 stars'
  alpha_post = alpha_prior + positive
  beta_post  = beta_prior + (total - positive)
  mean = alpha_post / (alpha_post + beta_post)
  # Lower confidence bound (95%)
  z = 1.96
  variance = alpha_post * beta_post / ((alpha_post+beta_post)^2 * (alpha_post+beta_post+1))
  lower_bound = mean - z * sqrt(variance)
  return lower_bound
入力
  • positive_reviews: int —
  • total_reviews: int —
出力
  • score: float —
Bench Evan Miller benchmark — Reddit / Amazon style sorting
📄 参考実装/論文

Google Business Profile Completeness Score

GBP 完成度スコア local_vertical_multimodal evaluation 2021

GBP 完成度の 100点スコア。photos 50+ / response rate 90%+ / posts 4+/月 が Local Pack 上位に強相関。

$$\text{GBP}(p) = \sum_{f \in \text{req}} 10 \cdot \mathbb{I}[p.f] + \sum_{f \in \text{opt}} 5 \cdot \mathbb{I}[p.f] + \sum_{f \in \text{prem}} 3 \cdot \mathbb{I}[p.f] + B_{photo} + B_{response} + B_{posts}$$
Time O(1) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function gbp_completeness(profile):
  required = ['name', 'address', 'phone', 'website', 'category', 'hours']
  optional = ['description', 'services', 'attributes', 'menu', 'photos', 'posts']
  premium = ['products', 'reviews_response_rate', 'video', 'q_and_a']

  score = 0
  for field in required:
    if getattr(profile, field): score += 10
  for field in optional:
    if getattr(profile, field): score += 5
  for field in premium:
    if getattr(profile, field): score += 3

  # Bonus for photos
  if len(profile.photos) >= 50: score += 10
  if profile.review_response_rate > 0.9: score += 10
  if len(profile.posts_last_30_days) >= 4: score += 5

  return min(score, 100)
入力
  • gbp_profile: GBPProfile —
出力
  • score: int — 0..100
Bench —

Haversine Distance for Local Ranking

ハバーサイン距離 (ローカルランキング) local_vertical_multimodal ranking 1801

球面三角法による地点間距離。Google Local Pack / Yelp / 食べログのローカルランキングで proximity decay として使用。

$$d = 2R \arcsin\sqrt{\sin^2\bigl(\frac{\Delta\phi}{2}\bigr) + \cos\phi_1 \cos\phi_2 \sin^2\bigl(\frac{\Delta\lambda}{2}\bigr)}$$
Time O(1) per pair Space O(1)
擬似コード
function haversine(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2):
  R = 6371  # Earth radius in km
  phi1, phi2 = radians(lat1), radians(lat2)
  dphi = radians(lat2 - lat1)
  dlambda = radians(lon2 - lon1)
  a = sin(dphi/2)^2 + cos(phi1)*cos(phi2)*sin(dlambda/2)^2
  c = 2 * atan2(sqrt(a), sqrt(1-a))
  return R * c  # km

function local_score(query, business, user_loc):
  rel = text_relevance(query, business.profile)
  dist = haversine(user_loc.lat, user_loc.lon, business.lat, business.lon)
  prox = exp(-dist / 5.0)  # decay over 5km
  rating = business.rating / 5.0
  return rel * prox * (0.6 + 0.4 * rating)
入力
  • lat/lon pairs: (float, float) —
出力
  • distance_km: float —
Bench Google Maps API uses similar — Standard for Geographic distance
📄 参考実装/論文

Local Pack Proximity-Relevance-Prominence

Local Pack PRP モデル local_vertical_multimodal ranking 2018

Google 公式の Local Pack ランキング 3 軸。距離 5km decay + relevance + prominence (reviews + GBP completeness + backlinks)。

$$R_{\text{local}} = \mathrm{rel}(q, b) \cdot e^{-d/\tau} \cdot \mathrm{prominence}(b)$$
Time O(1) per pair Space O(1)
擬似コード
function local_rank(query, business, user_location):
  # Google 公式: relevance × distance × prominence
  relevance = text_match(query, business.profile + business.categories)
  distance_km = haversine(user_location, business.geo)
  proximity = exp(-distance_km / 5.0)  # 5km decay
  prominence = (
    log(1 + business.review_count) * 0.3 +
    business.avg_rating / 5.0 * 0.2 +
    log(1 + business.backlink_count) * 0.2 +
    business.gbp_completeness / 100 * 0.3
  )
  return relevance * proximity * prominence
入力
  • query: str —
  • business: LocalBusiness —
  • user_loc: GeoPoint —
出力
  • score: float —
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

NAP Citation Consistency Check

NAP 引用一貫性チェック local_vertical_multimodal evaluation 2014

Yelp/Tabelog/業界ディレクトリの NAP (Name/Address/Phone) 不一致を検出。Moz Local / BrightLocal で自動。

$$r_{NAP}(b) = 1 - \frac{|\{(c, f) : c \in \text{citations}(b), f \in \{N, A, P\}, c.f \ne b.f\}|}{3 \cdot |\text{citations}(b)|}$$
Time O(|citations|) Space O(|inconsistencies|)
擬似コード
function check_nap_consistency(business):
  primary = {
    'name': normalize(business.name),
    'address': normalize_address(business.address),
    'phone': normalize_phone(business.phone)
  }
  citations = fetch_citations(business)  # Yelp/Tabelog/業界ディレクトリ
  inconsistencies = []
  for cit in citations:
    for field in ['name', 'address', 'phone']:
      if normalize(cit[field]) != primary[field]:
        inconsistencies.append({'source': cit.source, 'field': field,
                                'expected': primary[field], 'actual': cit[field]})
  consistency_rate = 1 - len(inconsistencies) / (len(citations) * 3)
  return consistency_rate, inconsistencies
入力
  • business: LocalBusiness —
出力
  • rate: float —
  • issues: List —
Bench —

function generate_review_response(review, business, llm):

Service Area Business Optimization

出張型ビジネス最適化 local_vertical_multimodal ranking 2017

出張型ビジネス (配管/鍵/モバイルサービス) の Local SEO。SAB は住所非公開 + Service Area + Location Pages。

$$\text{SAB}_{config} = \{\text{areas}: A_{\le 20}, \text{category}: c_{SAB}, \text{landing-pages}: \{p_a : a \in A, \text{content}_a \text{ unique}\}\}$$
Time O(|areas|) Space O(|areas|)

function optimize_sab(business, service_areas):
  # SAB (Service Area Business): plumber, locksmith, mobile service
  # Cannot list physical address on GBP, must use service area
  config = {
    'gbp_service_areas': service_areas,  # up to 20 cities/regions
    'category': pick_primary_sab_category(business),
    'service_pages': []
  }
  # Generate location-specific service pages (NOT doorways!)
  for area in service_areas:
    page = {
      'url': f'/services/{area.slug}/',
      'h1': f'{business.service} in {area.name}',
      'unique_content': generate_unique_local_content(area, business),  # 500+ words
      'reviews_from_area': filter_reviews_by_area(business.reviews, area),
      'schema': {'@type': 'Service', 'areaServed': area.schema}
    }
    config['service_pages'].append(page)
  return config

BERT for Passage Ranking (monoBERT)

monoBERT 通過ランキング neural_ir ranking 2019

Nogueira-Cho (2019) の BERT pointwise reranker。MS MARCO で当時の SoTA。Cross-encoder の代表。

$$s(q,d) = \mathbf{w}^\top \mathrm{BERT}_{\mathrm{[CLS]}}([\mathrm{CLS}], q, [\mathrm{SEP}], d, [\mathrm{SEP}])$$
Time O(L^2 * D) per (query, doc) pair L=512, D=hidden Space O(model_params ~ 110M for BERT-base)
擬似コード
function monoBERT_score(query, passage, model):
  input = "[CLS] " + query + " [SEP] " + passage + " [SEP]"
  tokens = tokenize(input, max_len=512)
  output = model.forward(tokens)
  cls_vec = output.last_hidden_state[0]  # CLS token
  score = linear(cls_vec)  # single scalar
  return score

BGE-Reranker

BGE Reranker (Cross-Encoder) neural_ir ranking 2023

BGE 系列の cross-encoder reranker。Top-100 → Top-10 で精度大幅改善。

$$s(q, p) = \sigma(\mathbf{w}^\top \text{BGE-CrossEnc}([CLS], q, [SEP], p))$$
Time O(L^2 d) per pair Space O(model_params)
擬似コード
function BGE_rerank(query, passages, model):
  scored = []
  for p in passages:
    input = f"{query} [SEP] {p}"
    score = model.classify(input)  # Cross-encoder logit
    scored.append((p, score))
  return sorted(scored, key=lambda x: -x[1])

$$\mathcal{L}_{\text{CLIP}} = -\frac{1}{2N} \sum_i [\log \frac{e^{s_{ii}}}{\sum_j e^{s_{ij}}} + \log \frac{e^{s_{ii}}}{\sum_j e^{s_{ji}}}]$$

ColBERT Late Interaction

ColBERT neural_ir retrieval 2020

Khattab-Zaharia (2020) Stanford の late-interaction モデル。各クエリトークンが各文書トークンと dot-product、MaxPool で集約。

$$s_{\mathrm{ColBERT}}(q, d) = \sum_{i=1}^{|q|} \max_{j=1..|d|} \mathbf{q}_i^\top \mathbf{d}_j$$
Time O(|q| * |d| * dim) per pair Space O(|d| * dim) per doc in index
擬似コード
function ColBERT_score(query, doc, model):
  q_tokens = model.encode_query(query)   # shape [|q|, dim]
  d_tokens = model.encode_doc(doc)        # shape [|d|, dim]
  # MaxSim: each query token finds its best match in doc
  score = 0
  for q_vec in q_tokens:
    max_sim = max(dot(q_vec, d_vec) for d_vec in d_tokens)
    score += max_sim
  return score
入力
  • query: str —
  • doc: str —
出力
  • score: float —
Bench MS MARCO + TREC DL — MRR@10 = 0.36 on MS MARCO (v1)
📄 参考実装/論文

ColBERTv2

ColBERTv2 neural_ir retrieval 2022

Santhanam et al. (2022) NAACL の ColBERT 改良。Centroid Interaction で index size 70% 縮減。

$$s_{\text{ColBERTv2}}(q,d) = \sum_{i=1}^{|q|} \max_{j} (q_i \cdot d_j), \quad d_j = c_{k(j)} + r_j$$
Time O(|q| * |d| * dim) score Space O(|d| * dim) per doc (compressed)
擬似コード
function colbertv2_score(q, d):
  q_emb = encode_query(q)  # [|q|, dim]
  d_emb = encode_doc(d)     # [|d|, dim]
  # Centroid Interaction (PLAID): residual + cluster
  s = sum(max(q_i.dot(d_j) for d_j in d_emb) for q_i in q_emb)
  return s

DPR (Dense Passage Retrieval)

DPR neural_ir retrieval 2020

Karpukhin et al. (2020) Facebook AI の bi-encoder. BM25 を上回る最初の純粋 dense retrieval。

$$\mathcal{L} = -\log \frac{e^{\mathrm{sim}(q, d^+)}}{e^{\mathrm{sim}(q, d^+)} + \sum_{j=1}^{n} e^{\mathrm{sim}(q, d^-_j)}}$$
Time Train O(B * (T_q + T_d)), Infer O(1) per query (ANN) Space O(|corpus| * dim) for index (~30GB for Wikipedia)
擬似コード
function DPR_encode_train(positive_pairs, hard_negs):
  for (q, d_pos, [d_neg_1..d_neg_n]) in batch:
    h_q = bert_q.encode(q)
    h_d_pos = bert_d.encode(d_pos)
    h_d_negs = [bert_d.encode(d) for d in d_neg_*]
    # In-batch negatives + explicit hard negatives
    logits = [dot(h_q, h_d_pos)] + [dot(h_q, h) for h in h_d_negs]
    loss = -log(softmax(logits)[0])  # positive at index 0
  return bert_q, bert_d

function DPR_retrieve(query, faiss_index):
  h_q = bert_q.encode(query)
  scores, indices = faiss_index.search(h_q, k=100)
  return [(corpus[i], scores[i]) for i in indices]
入力
  • query: str —
  • passage_index: FAISS index —
出力
  • top_k: List[(passage, score)] —
Bench Natural Questions Open — Top-100 retrieval: 79.4% on NQ-open

Dense + Lexical Fusion (Hybrid Score)

function dense_bm25_fusion(q, dense_index, bm25_index, alpha=0.5):

  bm25_results = bm25_index.search(q, k=100)
  # Convert both to common score scale via min-max
  return rrf([dense_results, bm25_results], k=60)

Federated Embedding Search

Multi-tenant SaaS の federated search。各 tenant のデータを混在させずに全体検索。

$$\text{top-k}_{\text{global}} = \text{Agg}(\{\text{top-k}_{\text{shard}_i}\}_{i=1}^N)$$

擬似コード
function federated_embed_search(client_query, federated_index):
  # Aggregate from multiple shards without exposing individual data
  encrypted_results = []
  for shard in federated_index:
    enc_local_result = shard.search_encrypted(client_query)
    encrypted_results.append(enc_local_result)
  return aggregate_and_decrypt(encrypted_results)

HNSW (Hierarchical Navigable Small World)

HNSW neural_ir indexing 2018

Malkov-Yashunin (2018) の ANN data structure。Faiss / Qdrant / Weaviate で標準。

$$\text{recall} \approx 1 - \exp(-c \cdot ef), \quad \text{search} = O(\log N)$$
Time O(log N) per query Space O(N * M)
擬似コード
function hnsw_search(query, graph, ef=50):
  # 1. From entry point, descend levels greedily
  curr = entry_point
  for level in range(graph.max_level, 0, -1):
    curr = greedy_search(query, curr, level)
  # 2. At level 0, beam search with ef
  return beam_search(query, curr, ef)

function fhe_search(encrypted_query, encrypted_index, fhe_params):

IVF (Inverted File) Index

IVF Index neural_ir indexing 2010

Jegou et al. (2010) IVF-ADC。Faiss の基本構造。Product Quantization 統合可。

$$\text{IVF}: \mathbf{v} \to \arg\min_c \|\mathbf{v} - \mathbf{c}\|, \quad \text{search } O(nprobe \cdot N/n_{\text{lists}})$$
Time O(nprobe * N/n_lists) Space O(N + n_lists)
擬似コード
function ivf_index(vectors, n_lists=1024):
  centroids = kmeans(vectors, n_lists)
  inverted = defaultdict(list)
  for v in vectors:
    closest = nearest_centroid(v, centroids)
    inverted[closest].append(v)
  return centroids, inverted

function ivf_search(query, centroids, inverted, nprobe=10):
  closest_centroids = top_k_centroids(query, centroids, nprobe)
  candidates = [v for c in closest_centroids for v in inverted[c]]
  return top_k(exact_distance(query, candidates))
入力
  • input: any — see description
出力
  • output: any — see description
Bench SIFT 1M — Recall 0.95

    return mean_pool(output)
  elif mode == 'sparse':
    return relu(linear(output)) # SPLADE-style
  elif mode == 'multi-vector':
    return output.token_embeddings  # ColBERT-style

Product Quantization

Product Quantization (PQ) neural_ir indexing 2011

Jegou et al. (2011) の vector compression。768-dim float32 → 64-byte。Faiss/Vespa で運用。

$$\mathbf{v} \approx [\mathbf{c}_1; ...; \mathbf{c}_M], \quad \text{compression} = 32d / 8M$$
Time O(M) encode Space O(M * 256 * d/M)
擬似コード
function pq_encode(vector, codebooks, n_subspaces=8):
  subvectors = split(vector, n_subspaces)
  codes = []
  for i, sv in enumerate(subvectors):
    code = nearest_codeword(sv, codebooks[i])  # 8-bit
    codes.append(code)
  return codes  # 8 bytes total

RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation)

RAG neural_ir retrieval 2020

Lewis et al. (2020) Facebook AI のオリジナル RAG。Perplexity / ChatGPT browsing / Bing Copilot の基盤。

$$p(y|x) = \sum_{z \in \mathrm{top-k}} p_\eta(z|x) \cdot p_\theta(y|x, z)$$
Time O(retrieve + generate) Space O(corpus_index + LLM)
擬似コード
function RAG_answer(query, retriever, generator):
  # 1. Retrieve top-k relevant passages
  passages = retriever.search(query, k=5)
  # 2. Concatenate as context
  context = "\n\n".join([p.text for p in passages])
  prompt = f"""
  Context:
  {context}

  Question: {query}
  Answer (cite sources):
  """
  # 3. Generate grounded answer
  answer = generator.generate(prompt)
  return answer, passages  # citations

RetroMAE Pretraining

RetroMAE 事前学習 neural_ir retrieval 2022

Xiao et al. (2022) EMNLP の Asymmetric Masked Autoencoder で IR 特化事前学習。BGE/E5 の基盤。

$$\mathcal{L}_{\text{RetroMAE}} = -\sum_{t \in \mathcal{M}} \log P(x_t | \text{enc}(\tilde{x}), \tilde{x}_{\setminus \mathcal{M}}^{dec})$$
Time O(L^2 d) per step Space O(model_params)
擬似コード
function RetroMAE_pretrain(corpus, model):
  for doc in corpus:
    # Asymmetric encoder-decoder
    masked_doc = mask(doc, ratio=0.15)
    encoder_out = encoder(masked_doc)  # 12-layer BERT
    decoder_out = decoder(encoder_out, masked_doc, ratio=0.5)  # 1-layer
    loss = reconstruction_loss(decoder_out, doc)
    optimizer.step(loss)

SPLADE Sparse Expansion

SPLADE neural_ir retrieval 2021

Formal et al. (2021) Naver Labs Europe の sparse + neural ハイブリッド。BM25 と inverted index で運用可能ながら BERT 並み精度。

$$w_j = \log\bigl(1 + \mathrm{ReLU}\bigl(\max_{i \in \mathrm{tokens}} \mathbf{W}_j \mathbf{h}_i\bigr)\bigr)$$
Time O(L * |V|) encode + sparse-dot retrieval Space Sparse (~100-200 active dims)
擬似コード
function SPLADE_encode(text, model):
  # MLM head predicts term importance over entire vocabulary
  logits = model.forward(text)  # [seq_len, |V|]
  # Aggregate over tokens with log-saturation
  rep = log(1 + ReLU(max_pool(logits, dim=0)))  # [|V|]
  return rep  # sparse vector

function SPLADE_score(query, doc, model):
  q_rep = SPLADE_encode(query, model)
  d_rep = SPLADE_encode(doc, model)
  return dot(q_rep, d_rep)
入力

出力
  • sparse_vec: Dict[term_id, weight] —

BERT-based Spell Correction

BERT-Spell query_understanding query_processing 2020

Pretrained BERT を spell correction に適用。Pinyin / Chinese で標準。

$$\hat{c}_i = \arg\max_{c} P_{\text{BERT}}(c | \text{context}_{i})$$
Time O(L^2 d) Space O(model_params)
擬似コード
function bert_spell(text):
  # Mask possible errors, predict
  candidates = []
  for i, char in enumerate(text):
    if confidence(char) < threshold:
      masked = text[:i] + '[MASK]' + text[i+1:]
      pred = bert.predict_mask(masked)
      candidates.append((i, char, pred))
  return apply_corrections(text, candidates)

HyDE (Hypothetical Document Embeddings)

HyDE query_understanding retrieval 2023

$$\hat{d} = \mathrm{LLM}(q), \quad \mathrm{retrieve}(q) = \mathrm{ANN}(\mathrm{enc}(\hat{d}))$$
Time O(LLM_latency + ANN_search) Space O(corpus * dim)
擬似コード
function HyDE_retrieve(query, llm, retriever):
  # 1. Generate hypothetical answer document
  prompt = "Write a passage that answers: " + query
  hypo_doc = llm.generate(prompt, max_tokens=200)
  # 2. Embed the hypothetical doc (not the query)
  h_hypo = encoder.encode(hypo_doc)
  # 3. Retrieve real docs nearest to hypothetical embedding
  return retriever.search(h_hypo, k=10)

Levenshtein Edit Distance

Levenshtein 編集距離 query_understanding query_processing 1965

Levenshtein (1965) 動的計画法による文字列編集距離。Spell correction / Fuzzy search の基礎。

$$D(i,j) = \min\begin{cases} D(i-1,j) + 1 \\ D(i,j-1) + 1 \\ D(i-1,j-1) + [s_i \neq t_j] \end{cases}$$
Time O(m * n) Space O(min(m,n)) with row optimization
擬似コード
function edit_distance(s1, s2):
  m, n = len(s1), len(s2)
  D = [[0]*(n+1) for _ in range(m+1)]
  for i in range(m+1): D[i][0] = i
  for j in range(n+1): D[0][j] = j
  for i in 1..m:
    for j in 1..n:
      cost = 0 if s1[i-1] == s2[j-1] else 1
      D[i][j] = min(
        D[i-1][j] + 1,      # deletion
        D[i][j-1] + 1,      # insertion
        D[i-1][j-1] + cost  # substitution
      )
  return D[m][n]
入力
  • s1: str —
  • s2: str —
出力
  • distance: int —
Bench Spell correction benchmarks — Used in Lucene FuzzyQuery
📄 参考実装/論文

Markov Chain Query Suggester

Markov Suggester query_understanding query_processing 2008

Mei et al. (2008) のクエリログ Markov chain。Suggest の基本実装。

$$P(q' | q) = \frac{\#\{(q, q') \in \text{sessions}\}}{\#\{q \in \text{sessions}\}}$$
Time O(|candidates|) Space O(|queries|^2) max
擬似コード
function markov_suggest(query, transition_matrix, k=10):
  # P(next | current) from session logs
  candidates = transition_matrix[query]
  return top_k(candidates, k)

Neural Suggester query_understanding query_processing 2018

$$P(q' | q) = \prod_{t} P(q'_t | q'_{<t}, q)$$

function neural_suggest(query, model):
  # Seq2seq with attention or Transformer decoder
  q_emb = encoder(query)
  candidates = decoder.generate(q_emb, beam_size=10)
  return candidates

Personalized Query Suggestion

Personalized Suggest query_understanding query_processing 2014

User profile を組み込んだ suggest。Google Personalized Search / Twitter で運用。

$$s(q' | q, u) = \lambda \cdot s_{\text{global}}(q') + (1-\lambda) \cdot s_{\text{user}}(q', u)$$
Time O(|candidates|) Space O(|user_profile|)
擬似コード
function personalized_suggest(query, user_profile, k=10):
  candidates = base_suggester(query)
  user_score = lambda q: cosine(q.embedding, user_profile.preference)
  reranked = sorted(candidates, key=lambda c: -(0.7 * c.global_score + 0.3 * user_score(c)))
  return reranked[:k]
