Phonetic + Edit Hybrid Spell

Phonetic Hybrid Spell query_understanding query_processing 2010

Soundex (Russell-Odell 1918) + Levenshtein (Wagner-Fischer 1974) の hybrid。人名検索で有効。

$$\text{score}(c) = \alpha \cdot \mathbb{I}[\text{soundex}(c) = \text{soundex}(t)] + (1-\alpha) \cdot \text{edit-dist}(c, t)$$
Time O(|dict|) Space O(|dict|)
擬似コード
function phonetic_edit_spell(typo, dictionary):
  candidates = []
  typo_soundex = soundex(typo)
  for word in dictionary:
    if soundex(word) == typo_soundex:
      candidates.append((word, levenshtein(typo, word)))
  return sorted(candidates, key=lambda x: x[1])[:5]

Query Intent Classification (BERT)

クエリ意図分類 (BERT) query_understanding query_processing 2019

Broder (2002) の navigational/informational/transactional の3分類を BERT で実装。RAG / Featured Snippet / Shopping trigger の前段。

$$P(c | q) = \mathrm{softmax}(\mathbf{W} \cdot \mathrm{BERT}_{\mathrm{[CLS]}}(q))_c$$
Time O(L^2 * D) per query Space O(model_params)
擬似コード
function classify_intent(query, model):
  tokens = tokenize("[CLS] " + query + " [SEP]")
  output = model.forward(tokens)
  cls = output.last_hidden_state[0]
  logits = classifier_head(cls)  # [n_classes]
  return softmax(logits).argmax()
  # Classes: navigational / informational / transactional / commercial

Query Suggestion via Clickthrough

クリックスルー由来 Query Suggestion query_understanding query_processing 2008

ユーザーの連続クエリパターンから suggestion graph を構築。Google/Bing の autosuggest の基本。

$$P(q' | q) = \frac{\#\{(q, q') \in \mathrm{co-sessions}\}}{\#\{q \in \mathrm{sessions}\}}$$
Time O(|sessions|) build, O(deg(q)) query Space O(|edges|)
擬似コード
function build_suggestion_graph(click_logs):
  G = WeightedGraph()
  for session in group_by_session(click_logs):
    queries = [event.query for event in session]
    for q1, q2 in consecutive_pairs(queries):
      G.add_edge(q1, q2, weight += 1)
  return G

function suggest(query, G, top_k=10):
  candidates = G.neighbors(query, sort_by=weight, desc=True)
  return candidates[:top_k]

出力

Symmetric Spelling Correction (SymSpell)

SymSpell スペル補正 query_understanding query_processing 2012

Garbe (2012) の Delete-only Symmetric Spelling Correction。Lucene FuzzyQuery より3桁高速。検索 typo 補正の業界標準。

$$\text{candidates}(q) = \bigcup_{d \in \text{deletes}(q, k)} \text{index}[d], \quad k = \text{max\_edit\_distance}$$
Time O(1) per lookup (vs O(|V|*|q|) for naive) Space O(|V| * |q|^max_edit)
擬似コード
function build_symspell_index(dictionary, max_edit=2):
  index = defaultdict(set)
  for word in dictionary:
    for delete_word in generate_deletes(word, max_edit):
      index[delete_word].add(word)
  return index

function symspell_correct(query, index, max_edit=2):
  candidates = set()
  for delete_query in generate_deletes(query, max_edit):
    candidates |= index.get(delete_query, set())
  return sorted(candidates, key=lambda c: edit_distance(query, c))[:5]
入力

  • dictionary: Set[str] —
出力
  • suggestions: List[str] —

Core Web Vitals (LCP/CLS/INP)

Core Web Vitals 計測 technical_seo evaluation 2020

Google 2020年5月発表。LCP <2.5s / CLS <0.1 / INP <200ms (2024年3月 FID→INP 置換) でランキング要因。web-vitals JS で計測。

$$\text{LCP} \le 2500\text{ms}, \quad \text{CLS} \le 0.1, \quad \text{INP} \le 200\text{ms} \quad \text{(p75 mobile)}$$
Time O(events) Space O(events)
擬似コード
function measure_CWV(page):
  # LCP: time until largest contentful paint
  lcp_observer = PerformanceObserver(type='largest-contentful-paint')
  lcp = lcp_observer.startTime  # ms

  # CLS: cumulative layout shift (unitless)
  cls = 0
  cls_observer = PerformanceObserver(type='layout-shift', callback=(entries) => {
    for (entry of entries) {
      if (!entry.hadRecentInput) cls += entry.value
    }
  })

  # INP: interaction to next paint (ms, p95 across all interactions)
  interactions = []
  inp_observer = PerformanceObserver(type='event', callback=(entries) => {
    for (entry of entries) interactions.push(entry.duration)
  })
  inp = percentile(interactions, 95)

  return {
    'LCP': lcp,                              # good <2500ms, poor >4000ms
    'CLS': cls,                              # good <0.1,    poor >0.25
    'INP': inp,                              # good <200ms,  poor >500ms
  }
入力
  • page_load_events: PerformanceObserver —
出力
  • metrics: Dict[LCP|CLS|INP, float] —
Bench CrUX BigQuery dataset — 75th percentile thresholds (mobile)
📄 参考実装/論文

Critical CSS Extraction

Critical CSS 抽出 technical_seo indexing 2014

Above-the-fold rendering を高速化するため初期 CSS を inline 化、残りを async load。LCP 改善の標準手法。

$$\text{CriticalCSS}(p) = \{r \in \text{CSS}(p) : \exists e \in \text{DOM}(p, \text{viewport}) \cap \text{matches}(r, e)\}$$
Time O(|nodes| * |rules|) Space O(|css|)
擬似コード
function extract_critical_css(html, full_css, viewport={1280, 800}):
  # 1. Render page in headless browser
  page = render(html, full_css, viewport)
  # 2. Identify above-the-fold DOM nodes
  visible_nodes = page.query_selector_all_visible_in_viewport()
  # 3. Match CSS rules that apply to visible nodes
  used_selectors = set()
  for rule in parse(full_css).rules:
    if any(node.matches(rule.selector) for node in visible_nodes):
      used_selectors.add(rule)
  # 4. Emit minimal CSS
  critical = ''.join(rule.text for rule in used_selectors)
  return critical, full_css  # inline critical, defer rest
入力
  • html: str —
  • css: str —
出力
  • critical_css: str —
  • deferred_css: str —
Bench PageSpeed Insights — Tools: critical / penthouse
📄 参考実装/論文

Font Loading Optimization (font-display)

フォント読込最適化 technical_seo indexing 2017

font-display: swap で FOIT 回避 / preload + crossorigin / unicode-range で必要文字のみ。CLS 0 維持で LCP 改善。

$$\text{FOIT/FOUT}: \text{font-display}: \text{swap} \lor \text{optional}, \quad \text{ascent-override}, \text{descent-override}, \text{size-adjust for fallback matching}$$
Time Eliminates FOIT Space Font file size
擬似コード
/* CSS */
@font-face {
  font-family: 'BrandFont';
  src: url('/fonts/brand.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-display: swap;  /* FOIT 回避: テキストすぐ表示 */
  font-weight: 100 900;  /* variable font */
  unicode-range: U+0020-007F, U+3000-30FF, U+4E00-9FFF;  /* 必要文字のみ */
}

/* HTML preload */
<link rel="preload" href="/fonts/brand.woff2" as="font" type="font/woff2" crossorigin>

/* Fallback metrics matching */
@font-face {
  font-family: 'BrandFont-Fallback';
  src: local('Hiragino Sans');
  ascent-override: 90%; descent-override: 20%; size-adjust: 105%;
}

Image Format Decision Tree (AVIF/WebP/JPEG)

画像フォーマット選択 technical_seo indexing 2020

AVIF (50% smaller than JPEG) → WebP fallback → JPEG fallback の picture element。Sharp/ImageMagick で一括変換。

$$\text{format}(i) = \begin{cases} \text{AVIF} & \text{if browser supports} \\ \text{WebP} & \text{else if browser supports} \\ \text{JPEG} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}$$
Time O(1) Space 3x file storage
擬似コード
function pick_image_format(image):
  # AVIF: best compression, 95%+ browser support (2025)
  # WebP: 98%+ browser support, fallback for AVIF
  # JPEG: universal fallback
  return f'''
<picture>
  <source type="image/avif" srcset="{image.avif}">
  <source type="image/webp" srcset="{image.webp}">
  <img src="{image.jpeg}" alt="{image.alt}"
       width="{image.width}" height="{image.height}"
       loading="lazy" decoding="async">
</picture>'''

Lazy Loading Critical Image Exclusion

重要画像の eager loading technical_seo indexing 2020

LCP 改善: 重要画像 (hero / above-fold / LCP 候補) は eager + fetchpriority=high + preload。残りは lazy。

$$\text{loading}(i) = \begin{cases} \text{eager}, \text{fetchpriority}=\text{high} & \text{if } i \in \text{above-fold} \cup \{i_{LCP}\} \\ \text{lazy}, \text{decoding}=\text{async} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}$$
Time O(|images|) Space O(|images|)
擬似コード
function optimize_image_loading(page):
  for img in page.images:
    if is_above_the_fold(img) or img == page.lcp_candidate:
      img.loading = 'eager'
      img.fetchpriority = 'high'
      # Preload hint
      page.head.add(f'<link rel="preload" as="image" href="{img.src}" fetchpriority="high">')
    else:
      img.loading = 'lazy'
      img.decoding = 'async'
    # Always set width/height to prevent CLS
    if not img.width or not img.height:
      img.width, img.height = probe_dimensions(img.src)
入力

出力
  • optimized_page: HTMLPage —

Resource Hints (preload/preconnect/prefetch)

Resource Hints 設定 technical_seo indexing 2014

W3C Resource Hints: preconnect (DNS+TLS) / preload (現在ページ必須) / prefetch (次ナビ予測)。LCP/INP 改善。

$$\text{hint} \in \{\text{preconnect: DNS+TLS}, \text{preload: critical asset}, \text{prefetch: next nav}, \text{dns-prefetch}\}$$
Time O(|hints|) Space O(|hints|)
擬似コード
function emit_resource_hints(page):
  hints = []
  # 1. preconnect: third-party origins
  for origin in critical_third_party_origins(page):
    hints.append(f'<link rel="preconnect" href="{origin}">')
    hints.append(f'<link rel="dns-prefetch" href="{origin}">')
  # 2. preload: critical assets (hero, fonts, critical JS)
  for asset in critical_assets(page):
    hints.append(f'<link rel="preload" as="{asset.type}" href="{asset.url}" {asset.crossorigin_attr}>')
  # 3. prefetch: likely next navigation
  for next_url in predicted_next_pages(page):
    hints.append(f'<link rel="prefetch" href="{next_url}">')
  return hints
入力

Service Worker Caching Strategy

Service Worker キャッシング technical_seo indexing 2015

Workbox の SWR (stale-while-revalidate) パターン。Mobile UX の決定的改善。PWA の基盤。

$$\text{response}(req) = \begin{cases} \text{cache.match}(req) & \text{if hit} \\ \text{fetch}(req) \to \text{cache.put} & \text{if miss} \end{cases} \quad \text{(SWR pattern)}$$
Time O(1) cache hit, O(network) miss Space O(|cached_assets|)
擬似コード
// Workbox stale-while-revalidate
import { registerRoute } from 'workbox-routing';
import { StaleWhileRevalidate, CacheFirst } from 'workbox-strategies';

// Static assets (cache-first, 1 year)
registerRoute(
  ({ request }) => ['style', 'script', 'image', 'font'].includes(request.destination),
  new CacheFirst({
    cacheName: 'static-v1',
    plugins: [new ExpirationPlugin({ maxAgeSeconds: 31536000 })]
  })
);

// HTML (stale-while-revalidate)
registerRoute(
  ({ request }) => request.mode === 'navigate',
  new StaleWhileRevalidate({ cacheName: 'pages-v1' })
);

Cloaking Detector

クローキング検出 web_spam evaluation 2010

User-Agent 別で異なるコンテンツを返す cloaking を検出。テキスト差分 + タイトル + ステータスコードで判定。

$$\text{IsCloaked}(u) = D_{text}(\text{HTML}_{bot}(u), \text{HTML}_{user}(u)) > 0.3 \lor \text{title}_{bot} \ne \text{title}_{user}$$
Time O(|html|) Space O(|html|)
擬似コード
function detect_cloaking(url):
  # Fetch as Googlebot vs as regular user
  googlebot_html = fetch(url, user_agent='Googlebot')
  user_html = fetch(url, user_agent='Mozilla/5.0 ...')
  # Compare DOM, text, key meta
  text_diff = 1 - difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, googlebot_html.text, user_html.text).ratio()
  title_match = googlebot_html.title == user_html.title
  status_diff = googlebot_html.status != user_html.status
  return text_diff > 0.3 or not title_match or status_diff
入力
  • url: str —
出力
  • is_cloaked: bool —
Bench —

Content Farm Detection

コンテンツファーム検出 web_spam evaluation 2011

Demand Media / eHow 等のコンテンツファーム検出。2011 Panda Update の検出対象。

$$\text{IsFarm}(d) = (\text{velocity}_d > 50 \land |\text{authors}_d| < 3) \lor (\text{thin\_pct}_d > 0.6 \land \text{ad\_density}_d > 0.02)$$
Time O(|pages|) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function detect_content_farm(domain):
  signals = {
    'avg_word_count': mean([p.word_count for p in domain.pages]),
    'duplicate_ratio': sum(p.duplicate_score for p in domain.pages) / len(domain.pages),
    'thin_pages_pct': count(p for p in domain.pages if p.word_count < 300) / len(domain.pages),
    'publish_velocity': pages_per_day(domain),
    'unique_authors': len(set(p.author for p in domain.pages)),
    'ad_density': mean(p.ad_blocks / p.word_count for p in domain.pages)
  }
  return (
    (signals['publish_velocity'] > 50 and signals['unique_authors'] < 3) or
    (signals['thin_pages_pct'] > 0.6 and signals['ad_density'] > 0.02)
  )
入力
  • domain: Domain —
出力
  • is_farm: bool —
Bench —

Doorway Page Detection

ドアウェイページ検出 web_spam evaluation 2015

Google 2015年3月の Doorway Update 検出ロジック。MinHash/LSH で大量類似ページを高速検出。

$$\text{IsDoorway}(p) = J_{sh}(p, p') > 0.7 \land |\text{url-variants}(p)| > 10 \land |\text{redirect-chain}(p)| > 0$$
Time O(|pages|^2) naive, O(|pages|) with LSH Space O(|pages|)
擬似コード
function detect_doorway(page, domain):
  # Doorways: thin pages targeting many keyword variants, redirecting to same destination
  shingle_sim = max(jaccard_shingles(page.text, other.text) for other in domain.pages if other != page)
  url_variants = count_similar_url_patterns(page.url, domain.urls)
  redirect_chain = follow_redirects(page.url)
  return (
    shingle_sim > 0.7 and  # high content duplication
    url_variants > 10 and   # mass-generated URL pattern
    len(redirect_chain) > 0 # redirects to other page
  )
入力
  • page: Page —
  • domain: Domain —
出力
  • is_doorway: bool —
Bench —

Expired Domain Abuse Detection (2024)

期限切れドメイン乱用検出 web_spam ranking 2024

Google 2024年3月導入。期限切れドメインを購入して別目的で再使用する手口を Wayback + 内容類似度で検出。

$$\text{IsExpAbused}(d) = \text{transfer}_{<365}(d) \land \cos(\vec{d}_{pre}, \vec{d}_{post}) < 0.3 \land \text{BL}_{pre} > 1000 \land \text{ad\_density}(d) > 0.05$$
Time O(|history|) Space O(|history|)
擬似コード
function detect_expired_domain_abuse(domain):
  whois_data = fetch_whois(domain)
  archive = fetch_wayback_history(domain)
  signals = {
    'recent_transfer': (today() - whois_data.last_transferred).days < 365,
    'topic_shift': cosine(embed(archive.pre_transfer.content),
                          embed(archive.post_transfer.content)) < 0.3,
    'historical_authority': max_backlinks_during_pre_transfer,
    'aggressive_monetization': ad_density(domain) > 0.05
  }
  return (
    signals['recent_transfer'] and
    signals['topic_shift'] and
    signals['historical_authority'] > 1000 and
    signals['aggressive_monetization']
  )
入力

Hidden Text Detection (CSS/JS)

隠しテキスト検出 web_spam evaluation 2005

CSS rendering 計算後の隠しテキストを検出。display:none / 同色 / position 飛ばし / 極小フォント を統合。

$$r_{hidden}(p) = \frac{\sum_{e \in \text{hidden}(p)} |\text{words}(e)|}{|\text{visible\_words}(p)| + 1}, \quad \text{hidden} = \{e : \text{display:none} \lor \text{color}=\text{bg} \lor \text{font-size} < 1\}$$
Time O(|dom|) Space O(|hidden|)
擬似コード
function detect_hidden_text(page):
  hidden = []
  for el in page.dom.iter():
    style = compute_style(el)
    if (style.display == 'none' or
        style.visibility == 'hidden' or
        style.font_size < 1 or
        (style.color == style.background_color) or
        style.position == 'absolute' and (style.left < -9999 or style.top < -9999)):
      hidden.append(el.text)
  hidden_word_count = sum(len(t.split()) for t in hidden)
  visible_word_count = page.visible_word_count
  return hidden_word_count / (visible_word_count + 1)
入力
  • page: Page —
出力
  • hidden_ratio: float —
Bench —

Link Spam Detection (Penguin-style)

リンクスパム検出 (Penguin型) web_spam ranking 2012

Google Penguin (2012) のリンクスパムシグナル。Exact-match anchor 過多 / 低品質ソース / 急速速度を統合検出。

$$S_{spam}(d) = 0.3 \cdot r_{exact} + 0.3 \cdot r_{low\_DA} + 0.2 \cdot \min(\frac{v_{recent}}{v_{baseline}}, 10)/10 + 0.2 \cdot r_{sitewide}$$
Time O(|backlinks|) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function detect_link_spam(domain):
  backlinks = fetch_backlinks(domain)
  signals = {
    'exact_match_anchor_pct': count(b for b in backlinks if b.anchor in domain.target_kws) / len(backlinks),
    'low_quality_source_pct': count(b for b in backlinks if domain_authority(b.source) < 10) / len(backlinks),
    'sudden_velocity': max(daily_backlink_count[-30:]) / max(daily_backlink_count[-180:-30], 1),
    'foreign_domain_pct': count(b for b in backlinks if b.source.tld != domain.tld) / len(backlinks),
    'sitewide_link_pct': count(b for b in backlinks if b.is_sitewide) / len(backlinks)
  }
  spam_score = (
    signals['exact_match_anchor_pct'] * 0.3 +
    signals['low_quality_source_pct'] * 0.3 +
    min(signals['sudden_velocity'], 10) / 10 * 0.2 +
    signals['sitewide_link_pct'] * 0.2
  )
  return spam_score
入力
  • domain: Domain —
出力
  • spam_score: float —
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

Site Reputation Abuse Detection (2024)

サイトレピュテーション乱用検出 web_spam ranking 2024

Google 2024年3月 Spam Update の新カテゴリ。第三者がドメイン評判を借りる手口を検出。

$$\text{IsAbused}(d) = r_{3rdparty}(d) > 0.3 \land \neg \text{editorial}(d) \land \frac{\text{diversity}_{recent}}{\text{diversity}_{baseline}} > 2.0$$
Time O(|pages|) Space O(|topics|)
擬似コード
function detect_reputation_abuse(domain):
  pages = domain.pages
  signals = {
    'editorial_oversight': has_editorial_policy(domain),
    'third_party_content_pct': count(p for p in pages if p.author.domain != domain) / len(pages),
    'topic_coherence': topic_clustering_silhouette(pages),
    'coupon_directory_count': count(p for p in pages if p.is_coupon_or_directory),
    'sudden_topic_diversification': topic_diversity_recent / topic_diversity_baseline
  }
  return (
    signals['third_party_content_pct'] > 0.3 and
    not signals['editorial_oversight'] and
    signals['sudden_topic_diversification'] > 2.0
  )
入力
  • domain: Domain —
出力
  • abuse_detected: bool —
Bench —

Sneaky Redirect Detection

偽装リダイレクト検出 web_spam evaluation 2010

Googlebot とユーザーで異なる最終 URL に redirect する手口を検出。Sneaky Redirect 違反。

$$\text{IsSneaky}(u) = \text{follow}(u, \text{UA}_{bot}) \ne \text{follow}(u, \text{UA}_{user})$$
Time O(redirect_chain) Space O(1)
擬似コード
function detect_sneaky_redirect(url, googlebot_ua, mobile_ua):
  bot_response = fetch(url, user_agent=googlebot_ua, follow_redirects=False)
  mobile_response = fetch(url, user_agent=mobile_ua, follow_redirects=False)
  desktop_response = fetch(url, user_agent='Mozilla/Desktop', follow_redirects=False)
  bot_final = follow_redirects(url, googlebot_ua)
  mobile_final = follow_redirects(url, mobile_ua)
  return bot_final != mobile_final  # Different destination = sneaky
入力
  • url: str —
出力
  • is_sneaky: bool —
Bench —
📄 参考実装/論文

TrustRank Algorithm

TrustRank web_spam ranking 2004

Gyöngyi-Garcia-Molina-Pedersen (2004) VLDB の信頼伝播ベース PageRank。Spam サイトを統計的に検出。

$$\mathbf{t}^{(k+1)} = (1-d) \cdot \mathbf{s} + d \cdot \mathbf{M}^\top \mathbf{t}^{(k)}$$
Time O(k * (|V| + |E|)) Space O(|V| + |E|)
擬似コード
function TrustRank(graph, seed_trusted, d=0.85, max_iter=100):
  N = |graph.nodes|
  # Initialize: trust = uniform over trusted seeds, 0 elsewhere
  trust = {v: 1/|seed_trusted| if v in seed_trusted else 0 for v in graph.nodes}
  for iter in range(max_iter):
    trust_new = {v: 0 for v in graph.nodes}
    for v in graph.nodes:
      if v in seed_trusted:
        trust_new[v] = (1-d) * (1/|seed_trusted|)
      for u in graph.in_edges(v):
        trust_new[v] += d * trust[u] / |graph.out_edges(u)|
    trust = trust_new
  return trust  # high = trusted, low = candidate spam
入力
  • graph: DirectedGraph —
  • trusted_seeds: Set[node] —
出力
  • trust_scores: Dict[node, float] —
Bench .gov + Yahoo Slurp — Combating Web Spam VLDB 2004

FAQ Accordion (details/summary)

FAQ アコーディオン card →FAQ Schema

FAQPage Schema + 2023年8月以降 PAA トリガー狙い。一般サイトは Rich Result 表示外だが PAA に依然有効。

使う場面 FAQ ページ / 製品 FAQ / サポート
CWV impact Neutral (CSS only animation)
A11y Native keyboard support, Enter/Space toggle
HTML テンプレート
<section class="faq" itemscope itemtype="https://schema.org/FAQPage">
  <h2>よくある質問</h2>

  <details itemscope itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question">
    <summary itemprop="name">○○について教えてください</summary>
    <div itemscope itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer">
      <div itemprop="text">
        <p>40-80字の回答文をここに。</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </details>

  <details itemscope itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question">
    <summary itemprop="name">別の質問</summary>
    <div itemscope itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer">
      <div itemprop="text"><p>回答</p></div>
    </div>
  </details>
</section>
CSS
.faq details { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); padding: 16px 0; }
.faq summary { cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600; padding: 8px 0; list-style: none; }
.faq summary::before { content: '+'; margin-right: 12px; transition: transform 0.2s; }
.faq details[open] summary::before { transform: rotate(45deg); }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .faq summary::before { transition: none; } }
ARIA Native <details>/<summary> = built-in ARIA

Toast Notification

Toast 通知 feedback →UX

WAI-ARIA Authoring Practices Toast。エラーは aria-live='assertive'。

使う場面 操作フィードバック (フォーム送信成功等)
CWV impact Neutral
A11y Auto-dismiss + screen reader announce
HTML テンプレート
<div role="status" aria-live="polite" class="toast-region">
  <!-- Toasts injected here via JS -->
</div>

<script>
function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
  const toast = document.createElement('div');
  toast.className = `toast toast-${type}`;
  toast.textContent = message;
  document.querySelector('.toast-region').appendChild(toast);
  setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 5000);
}
</script>
CSS
.toast-region { position: fixed; bottom: 24px; right: 24px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; z-index: 1000; }
.toast { padding: 12px 24px; background: var(--card); border-left: 3px solid var(--accent); box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.1); animation: slideIn 0.2s; }
.toast-success { border-left-color: green; }
.toast-error { border-left-color: red; }
@keyframes slideIn { from { transform: translateX(100%); } to { transform: translateX(0); } }
ARIA role='status', aria-live='polite' (or 'assertive' for errors)
📘 参考

Comment Form with Honeypot

コメントフォーム + Honeypot form →Spam Prevention

Comment Spam Bot 検出 (no CAPTCHA UX impact)。Honeypot + timing 両方で精度向上。

使う場面 コメント / フォーラム
CWV impact Server: reject if honeypot filled OR submit < 3s
A11y aria-hidden hides from screen readers
HTML テンプレート
<form action="/api/comment" method="post" class="comment-form">
  <label for="comment-name">名前
    <input id="comment-name" type="text" name="name" required autocomplete="name">
  </label>
  <label for="comment-text">コメント
    <textarea id="comment-text" name="text" required minlength="10" maxlength="1000"></textarea>
  </label>

  <!-- Honeypot field - hidden from real users, bots fill it -->
  <div aria-hidden="true" style="position:absolute;left:-9999px">
    <label for="website">Website (do not fill)</label>
    <input id="website" type="text" name="website" tabindex="-1" autocomplete="off">
  </div>

  <!-- Timing check via hidden timestamp -->
  <input type="hidden" name="form_loaded" value="{{ now_timestamp }}">

  <button type="submit">投稿</button>
</form>
CSS
.comment-form label { display: block; margin-bottom: 12px; }
.comment-form textarea { width: 100%; min-height: 120px; resize: vertical; }
ARIA aria-hidden + tabindex=-1 on honeypot

Estimated Reading Time

<script>
// Auto-compute: words / 250 (English) or chars / 600 (Japanese)
const text = document.querySelector('article').textContent;

Hero with Preloaded LCP Image

    <source type="image/webp" srcset="/hero.webp">

         loading="eager" fetchpriority="high" decoding="sync">
  </picture>

    <p class="lead">200-300字のリード文</p>

<div class="freshness-meta">
  <time datetime="2026-05-21" itemprop="dateModified">最終更新 2026-05-21</time>
  <span class="reviewed-by">
    Reviewed by <a href="/authors/dr-yamada">山田博士 (MD)</a>
  </span>
</div>
CSS
.freshness-meta { display: flex; gap: 16px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-muted);
  padding: 8px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); margin-bottom: 24px; }
.reviewed-by a { color: var(--accent); text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 1px dotted; }
ARIA <time datetime=...> for machine readability

Reading Progress Bar

読書進捗バー hero →Reader Experience

Dwell time + scroll depth p75 改善。passive scroll listener 必須。

使う場面 長文 (3000字+)、教科書、補論
CWV impact Neutral (CSS transform only)
A11y prefers-reduced-motion: { transition: none }
HTML テンプレート
<div class="reading-progress" role="progressbar" aria-label="読書進捗"
     aria-valuenow="0" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100">
  <div class="reading-progress-bar"></div>
</div>

<script>
const bar = document.querySelector('.reading-progress-bar');
const container = document.querySelector('.reading-progress');
window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
  const h = document.documentElement;
  const pct = (h.scrollTop / (h.scrollHeight - h.clientHeight)) * 100;
  bar.style.width = pct + '%';
  container.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', Math.round(pct));
}, { passive: true });
</script>
CSS
.reading-progress { position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; height: 3px; z-index: 1000; }
.reading-progress-bar { height: 100%; background: var(--accent); width: 0; transition: width 50ms; }
ARIA role='progressbar', aria-valuenow/min/max
📘 参考

Responsive Picture with AVIF/WebP

レスポンシブ Picture media →Image Pack

AVIF (50% smaller) → WebP (98% support) → JPEG fallback。srcset + sizes でデバイス別配信。

使う場面 全画像 (hero 除く)
CWV impact 30-50% bandwidth reduction
A11y alt for all (or '' if decorative)
HTML テンプレート
<picture>
  <source type="image/avif"
          srcset="/img/hero-400.avif 400w, /img/hero-800.avif 800w, /img/hero-1600.avif 1600w"
          sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 800px">
  <source type="image/webp"
          srcset="/img/hero-400.webp 400w, /img/hero-800.webp 800w, /img/hero-1600.webp 1600w"
          sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 800px">
  <img src="/img/hero-800.jpg"
       srcset="/img/hero-400.jpg 400w, /img/hero-800.jpg 800w, /img/hero-1600.jpg 1600w"
       sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 800px"
       alt="記述的な代替テキスト"
       width="1600" height="900"
       loading="lazy" decoding="async">
</picture>
CSS
.responsive-img { width: 100%; height: auto; aspect-ratio: 16/9; }
ARIA alt descriptive, width/height intrinsic, loading=lazy

Mobile Hamburger Menu

モバイルハンバーガーメニュー navigation →Mobile-First

Mobile-First Indexing 必須。hidden attribute で意味ロジック分離 (display:none より良い)。

使う場面 モバイル全サイト
CWV impact hidden attr prevents render
A11y Focus trap when open recommended
HTML テンプレート
<header>
  <button class="hamburger" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="mobile-nav"
          aria-label="メニューを開く">
    <span></span><span></span><span></span>
  </button>
  <nav id="mobile-nav" aria-label="モバイルメニュー" hidden>
    <ul>
      <li><a href="/">Home</a></li>
      <li><a href="/services">Services</a></li>
    </ul>
  </nav>
</header>

<script>
const btn = document.querySelector('.hamburger');
const nav = document.getElementById('mobile-nav');
btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const open = btn.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
  btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', !open);
  btn.setAttribute('aria-label', open ? 'メニューを開く' : 'メニューを閉じる');
  nav.hidden = open;
});
</script>
CSS
.hamburger { display: none; background: none; border: 0; padding: 12px; }
.hamburger span { display: block; width: 24px; height: 2px; background: var(--text); margin: 4px 0; }
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .hamburger { display: block; }
  #mobile-nav:not([hidden]) { position: fixed; inset: 56px 0 0; background: var(--bg); padding: 24px; }
}
ARIA aria-expanded, aria-controls, aria-label updates on toggle
📘 参考

Pagination with rel='prev/next'

ページネーション navigation →Indexing

rel='next/prev' は Google が2019年に廃止したが Bing/Yandex は継続使用。Schema は自己 canonical。

CWV impact Neutral
A11y Keyboard accessible, screen reader announces current
HTML テンプレート

<!-- in <body> -->
<nav aria-label="ページネーション" class="pagination">
  <ul>
    <li><a href="/blog/page/2/" rel="prev">← 前のページ</a></li>
    <li><a href="/blog/page/1/">1</a></li>
    <li><a href="/blog/page/2/">2</a></li>
    <li><span aria-current="page" aria-label="現在のページ、3ページ目">3</span></li>
    <li><a href="/blog/page/4/">4</a></li>
    <li><a href="/blog/page/4/" rel="next">次のページ →</a></li>
  </ul>
</nav>
CSS

.pagination a, .pagination span { padding: 8px 12px; border-radius: 4px; min-width: 40px; text-align: center; }

ARIA aria-label, aria-current, rel='prev'/'next' (Google ignored since 2019 but Bing uses)

Content Collections + Schema

Astro schema
編集場所 src/content/config.ts
import { defineCollection, z } from 'astro:content'

const blogCollection = defineCollection({
  type: 'content',
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string(),
    description: z.string(),
    publishDate: z.date(),
    updateDate: z.date().optional(),
    author: z.object({ name: z.string(), orcid: z.string().optional() }),
    image: z.object({ url: z.string(), alt: z.string() }),
    canonical: z.string().url().optional(),
  }),
})

export const collections = { blog: blogCollection }
期待効果 Frontmatter を Zod スキーマで型保証 + SEO 必須項目を強制
注意 schema 違反は build 失敗、CI で検出可能
📘 公式 doc

    sitemap({
      filter: (page) => !page.includes('/draft/'),
      customPages: ['https://example.com/api-generated/'],
      i18n: { defaultLocale: 'ja', locales: { ja: 'ja-JP', en: 'en-US' } },

Content model with SEO + GraphQL

Contentful schema
編集場所 queries/article.graphql
query GetArticle($slug: String!) {
  articleCollection(where: { slug: $slug }, limit: 1) {
    items {
      title
      slug
      excerpt
      sys { publishedAt firstPublishedAt }
      seoMetadata {
        metaTitle
        metaDescription
        canonicalUrl
        ogImage { url width height description }
        noIndex
        structuredDataJson
      }
      author { name orcidId linkedinUrl }
    }
  }
}
期待効果 Contentful GraphQL で SEO metadata を 1-query で取得
注意 rate limit 注意 (Free tier 7 req/sec)

Async view + CWV improvement

Django 5 indexing
編集場所 myapp/views.py
from django.views.decorators.cache import cache_page
from asgiref.sync import sync_to_async

@cache_page(60 * 60)  # 1 hour cache
async def article_detail(request, slug):
    article = await sync_to_async(Article.objects.get)(slug=slug)
    related = await sync_to_async(article.get_related)()
    return render(request, 'article.html', {'article': article, 'related': related})
期待効果 Django 5 async view で TTFB 改善 + cache_page で CDN-like
注意 DB ORM は sync_to_async で wrap (Django ORM は async 部分対応)
📘 公式 doc

Meta tags template tag

  <link rel="canonical" href="{% absolute_url 'article-detail' article.slug %}" />
  <meta property="og:title" content="{{ article.title }}" />
  <meta property="og:image" content="{{ article.image.url }}" />
  <script type="application/ld+json">
    {{ article.json_ld|safe }}
  </script>
{% endblock %}

Sitemap framework

Django 5 indexing
編集場所 myapp/sitemaps.py
from django.contrib.sitemaps import Sitemap
from .models import Article

class ArticleSitemap(Sitemap):
    changefreq = 'weekly'
    priority = 0.7
    protocol = 'https'

    def items(self):
        return Article.objects.filter(published=True)

    def lastmod(self, obj):
        return obj.updated_at

# urls.py
from django.contrib.sitemaps.views import sitemap
urlpatterns = [
    path('sitemap.xml', sitemap, {'sitemaps': {'articles': ArticleSitemap}}),
]
期待効果 Django 標準の sitemap framework で declarative
注意 DB query が走るので caching 推奨 (per_object_cache)
📘 公式 doc

robots.txt view

Django 5 crawling
編集場所 myapp/views.py
from django.http import HttpResponse

def robots_txt(request):
    lines = [
        "User-agent: *",
        "Disallow: /admin/",
        "Allow: /",
        "",
        "User-agent: GPTBot",
        "Disallow: /",
        "",
        "User-agent: anthropic-ai",
        "Disallow: /",
        "",
        f"Sitemap: {request.build_absolute_uri('/sitemap.xml')}",
    ]
    return HttpResponse("\n".join(lines), content_type="text/plain")
期待効果 View で動的 robots.txt + 環境別 URL
注意 build_absolute_uri で正しい canonical を生成
📘 公式 doc

Hybrid search with RRF

Elasticsearch retrieval
編集場所 elasticsearch_query.json
GET /products/_search
{
  "size": 10,
  "query": {
    "function_score": {
      "query": { "bool": { "should": [
        { "match": { "name": { "query": "...", "boost": 1.5 } } },
        { "match": { "description": "..." } }
      ]}},
      "functions": [{ "script_score": { "script": { "source": "doc['popularity'].value" } } }]
    }
  },
  "knn": {
    "field": "name_embedding",
    "query_vector": [0.1, 0.2, ...],
    "k": 100,
    "num_candidates": 1000
  },
  "rank": { "rrf": { "window_size": 100, "rank_constant": 60 } }
}

注意 8.8 未満は手動 RRF 実装必要

Members + Newsletter Schema

Ghost (Headless) schema

<script type="application/ld+json">
{
  "@context": "https://schema.org",
  "@type": "Article",
  "headline": "{{title}}",
  "datePublished": "{{date format='YYYY-MM-DD'}}",
  "dateModified": "{{updated_at format='YYYY-MM-DD'}}",
  "author": {
    "@type": "Person",
    "name": "{{primary_author.name}}",
    "sameAs": [
      {{#if primary_author.twitter}}"https://twitter.com/{{primary_author.twitter}}",{{/if}}
      {{#if primary_author.facebook}}"https://facebook.com/{{primary_author.facebook}}"{{/if}}
    ]
  },
  "publisher": {
    "@type": "Organization",
    "name": "{{@site.title}}",
    "logo": { "@type": "ImageObject", "url": "{{@site.logo}}" }
  },
  {{#if feature_image}}"image": "{{feature_image}}",{{/if}}
  "mainEntityOfPage": "{{url absolute='true'}}",
  "wordCount": "{{word_count}}",
  "articleBody": "{{plaintext}}"
}
</script>

Hugo Modules with Schema

Hugo schema
編集場所 layouts/_default/baseof.html
{{ $schema := dict
  "@context" "https://schema.org"
  "@graph" (slice
    (dict "@type" "Organization" "@id" (printf "%s#org" .Site.BaseURL) "name" .Site.Title)
    (dict "@type" "WebSite" "@id" (printf "%s#site" .Site.BaseURL) "publisher" (dict "@id" (printf "%s#org" .Site.BaseURL)))
    (dict "@type" "Article" "headline" .Title "datePublished" (.Date.Format "2006-01-02") "dateModified" (.Lastmod.Format "2006-01-02"))
  )
}}
<script type="application/ld+json">{{ $schema | jsonify | safeJS }}</script>

注意 safeJS は XSS 注意、Hugo の jsonify は安全

Image optimization with intervention/image

Laravel 11+ indexing
編集場所 app/Http/Controllers/MediaController.php
use Intervention\Image\ImageManager;
use Intervention\Image\Drivers\Gd\Driver;

public function transform(string $path, string $format) {
    $manager = new ImageManager(new Driver());
    $image = $manager->read(storage_path("app/{$path}"));

    return $image->encode(match($format) {
        'avif' => new \Intervention\Image\Encoders\AvifEncoder(quality: 75),
        'webp' => new \Intervention\Image\Encoders\WebpEncoder(quality: 80),
        default => new \Intervention\Image\Encoders\JpegEncoder(quality: 85),
    });
}

# Use with route: /img/{path}/{format} → cached via Cloudflare
期待効果 Intervention で AVIF/WebP 動的変換 + Cloudflare cache で高速化
注意 GD driver は AVIF 非対応、Imagick driver 必要
📘 公式 doc

Livewire form with honeypot

Laravel 11+ form
編集場所 app/Livewire/ContactForm.php
use Livewire\Component;
use Livewire\Attributes\Validate;

class ContactForm extends Component
{
    #[Validate('required|email')]
    public string $email = '';

    #[Validate('required|min:10')]
    public string $message = '';

    public string $website = '';  // Honeypot

    public int $formLoadedAt = 0;

    public function mount() { $this->formLoadedAt = time(); }

    public function submit() {
        if (!empty($this->website)) abort(403, 'spam');
        if ((time() - $this->formLoadedAt) < 3) abort(403, 'too fast');
        // ...
    }
}
期待効果 Livewire でリアルタイム validation + honeypot + timing
注意 Honeypot は CSS で hide、aria-hidden + tabindex=-1
📘 公式 doc

SEO with artesaos/seotools

Laravel 11+ schema
編集場所 app/Http/Controllers/BlogController.php
use Artesaos\SEOTools\Facades\SEOTools;

public function show(Post $post) {
    SEOTools::setTitle($post->title);
    SEOTools::setDescription($post->excerpt);
    SEOTools::setCanonical(route('blog.show', $post));
    SEOTools::opengraph()->setUrl(route('blog.show', $post));
    SEOTools::opengraph()->addImage($post->image_url);
    SEOTools::jsonLd()->setType('Article');
    SEOTools::jsonLd()->setTitle($post->title);
    return view('blog.show', compact('post'));
}
期待効果 artesaos/seotools で fluent API
注意 Blade view で {!! SEOTools::generate() !!} を出力
📘 公式 doc

Sitemap with spatie/laravel-sitemap

Laravel 11+ indexing
編集場所 app/Console/Commands/GenerateSitemap.php
use Spatie\Sitemap\SitemapGenerator;

public function handle() {
    SitemapGenerator::create(config('app.url'))
        ->configureCrawler(fn ($crawler) => $crawler->setMaximumCrawlCount(10000))
        ->hasCrawled(function ($url) {
            if (str_contains($url->url, '/admin')) return null;
            return $url;
        })
        ->writeToFile(public_path('sitemap.xml'));
}

# Schedule daily in app/Console/Kernel.php
$schedule->command('sitemap:generate')->daily();
期待効果 spatie/laravel-sitemap で crawl-based 生成 + cron で日次更新
注意 Crawl-based は大規模サイトでメモリ大量消費に注意
📘 公式 doc

Dynamic sitemap.xml

Next.js App Router indexing
編集場所 app/sitemap.ts
import { MetadataRoute } from 'next'

export default async function sitemap(): Promise<MetadataRoute.Sitemap> {
  const posts = await getAllPosts()
  return [
    { url: 'https://example.com', lastModified: new Date(), changeFrequency: 'daily', priority: 1.0 },
    ...posts.map(post => ({
      url: `https://example.com/${post.slug}`,
      lastModified: post.updatedAt,
      changeFrequency: 'weekly' as const,
      priority: 0.7,
    })),
  ]
}
期待効果 Next.js が /sitemap.xml を自動配信、build 時に static または revalidate
注意 Edge runtime では使えない、Node.js runtime のみ
📘 公式 doc

import { Noto_Sans_JP, Noto_Serif_JP } from 'next/font/google'

const notoSans = Noto_Sans_JP({
  subsets: ['latin'],
  weight: ['400', '500', '700'],
  display: 'swap',
  variable: '--font-sans',
})

const notoSerif = Noto_Serif_JP({
  subsets: ['latin'],
  weight: ['400', '700'],
  display: 'swap',
  variable: '--font-serif',
})

export default function RootLayout({ children }) {
  return (
    <html lang="ja" className={`${notoSans.variable} ${notoSerif.variable}`}>
      <body>{children}</body>
    </html>
  )
}

ISR for fresh content + canonical

編集場所 app/blog/[slug]/page.tsx
export const revalidate = 3600  // 1 hour

export async function generateStaticParams() {
  const posts = await getAllPosts()
  return posts.map(p => ({ slug: p.slug }))
}

export default async function BlogPost({ params }) {
  const post = await getPost(params.slug)
  if (!post) notFound()
  return <article>{post.content}</article>
}
期待効果 ISR で1時間ごとに re-generate、static のまま fresh content

JSON-LD @graph in Server Component

Next.js App Router schema
編集場所 app/[slug]/page.tsx
export default async function Page({ params }) {
  const post = await getPost(params.slug)
  const jsonLd = {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@graph': [
      { '@type': 'Organization', '@id': 'https://example.com/#org', name: '...', sameAs: [...] },
      { '@type': 'WebSite', '@id': 'https://example.com/#site', publisher: { '@id': 'https://example.com/#org' } },
      { '@type': 'Article', '@id': `https://example.com/${params.slug}#article`,
        headline: post.title, datePublished: post.publishedAt, dateModified: post.updatedAt,
        author: { '@type': 'Person', name: post.author.name },
        publisher: { '@id': 'https://example.com/#org' },
        mainEntityOfPage: `https://example.com/${params.slug}` },
      { '@type': 'BreadcrumbList', itemListElement: post.breadcrumbs.map((b, i) => ({
        '@type': 'ListItem', position: i + 1, name: b.name, item: b.url
      })) },
    ]
  }
  return (
    <>
      <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(jsonLd) }} />
      <article>{post.content}</article>
    </>
  )
}
期待効果 Server Component で SSR 時に inject (CSR でも search bot に確実)
注意 dangerouslySetInnerHTML だが JSON.stringify で XSS 安全
📘 公式 doc

Metadata API with OG + Twitter + canonical

Next.js App Router schema
編集場所 app/[slug]/page.tsx
import type { Metadata } from 'next'

export async function generateMetadata({ params }): Promise<Metadata> {
  const post = await getPost(params.slug)
  return {
    title: post.title,
    description: post.excerpt,
    alternates: { canonical: `https://example.com/${params.slug}` },
    openGraph: {
      title: post.title,
      description: post.excerpt,
      url: `https://example.com/${params.slug}`,
      images: [{ url: post.image, width: 1200, height: 630 }],
      locale: 'ja_JP',
      type: 'article',
      publishedTime: post.publishedAt,
      modifiedTime: post.updatedAt,
      authors: [post.author.name],
    },
    twitter: { card: 'summary_large_image', creator: '@yourhandle' },
    robots: { index: true, follow: true, googleBot: { 'max-snippet': -1, 'max-image-preview': 'large' } },
  }
}
期待効果 OG / Twitter / canonical / robots を Next.js Metadata API で一元管理
注意 generateMetadata は async / params は dynamic でも async resolver
📘 公式 doc

Server Action with form validation

Next.js App Router form
編集場所 app/contact/page.tsx
'use server'
async function submitContact(formData: FormData) {
  const email = formData.get('email')
  // Server-side validation + rate limit + honeypot check
  if (formData.get('website')) return { error: 'spam' }
  const elapsed = Date.now() - parseInt(formData.get('form_loaded'))
  if (elapsed < 3000) return { error: 'too_fast' }
  // ...
}

export default function ContactPage() {
  return (
    <form action={submitContact}>
      <input name="email" type="email" required autoComplete="email" />
      <div aria-hidden style={{position:'absolute',left:'-9999px'}}>
        <input name="website" tabIndex={-1} />
      </div>
      <input type="hidden" name="form_loaded" value={Date.now()} />
      <button>送信</button>
    </form>
  )
}
期待効果 Server Action + honeypot + timing で spam 防御 (CAPTCHA 不要)
注意 Date.now() は server clock skew に注意
📘 公式 doc

robots.ts with AI crawler block

import { MetadataRoute } from 'next'

export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  return {
    rules: [
      { userAgent: '*', allow: '/', disallow: ['/api/', '/admin/'] },
      { userAgent: 'GPTBot', disallow: '/' },
      { userAgent: 'ClaudeBot', disallow: '/' },
      { userAgent: 'anthropic-ai', disallow: '/' },
      { userAgent: 'CCBot', disallow: '/' },
      { userAgent: 'Google-Extended', disallow: '/' },
      { userAgent: 'Bytespider', disallow: '/' },
    ],
    sitemap: 'https://example.com/sitemap.xml',
  }
}
期待効果 robots.txt を Next.js App Router で動的生成
注意 Google-Extended は Gemini の学習 bot (AI Overviews 表示には影響しない)

robots.txt with controller

Rails 7+ crawling
編集場所 app/controllers/robots_controller.rb
class RobotsController < ApplicationController
  def show
    @rules = <<~ROBOTS
      User-agent: *
      Disallow: /admin/
      Allow: /

      User-agent: GPTBot
      Disallow: /

      User-agent: anthropic-ai
      Disallow: /

      Sitemap: #{ENV['HOST']}/sitemap.xml
    ROBOTS
    render plain: @rules, content_type: 'text/plain'
  end
end

# config/routes.rb
get '/robots.txt', to: 'robots#show'

meta export with merge

Remix schema
編集場所 app/routes/blog.$slug.tsx
import type { MetaFunction } from '@remix-run/node'

export const meta: MetaFunction<typeof loader> = ({ data, params }) => {
  if (!data) return [{ title: 'Not Found' }]
  return [
    { title: data.post.title },
    { name: 'description', content: data.post.excerpt },
    { property: 'og:title', content: data.post.title },
    { property: 'og:image', content: data.post.image },
    { tagName: 'link', rel: 'canonical', href: `https://example.com/blog/${params.slug}` },
  ]
}

export async function loader({ params }) {
  const post = await getPost(params.slug)
  if (!post) throw new Response(null, { status: 404 })
  return json({ post })
}
期待効果 Remix meta export で loader data からメタを生成
注意 tagName: 'link' で <link> tag 出力可能
📘 公式 doc

prefetch on hover/intent

import { Link } from '@remix-run/react'

// Prefetch on hover (intent) for high-conversion links

GROQ query for SEO metadata

Sanity Studio schema
編集場所 sanity/queries.ts
export const POST_SEO_QUERY = \`
  *[_type == "post" && slug.current == $slug][0] {
    title,
    "slug": slug.current,
    excerpt,
    publishedAt,
    _updatedAt,
    "author": author->{name, "orcid": coalesce(orcid, null), bio},
    "mainImage": mainImage.asset->{url, metadata{dimensions{width, height}, lqip}},
    "categories": categories[]->title,
    seo {
      metaTitle,
      metaDescription,
      canonical,
      noIndex,
      "ogImage": ogImage.asset->url
    }
  }
\`
期待効果 GROQ で SEO メタを project-level で集約取得
注意 coalesce で nullable 対応、lqip で blur placeholder

Structured Content for Schema.org

Sanity Studio schema
編集場所 sanity/schemas/post.ts
export default {
  name: 'post',
  type: 'document',
  fields: [
    { name: 'title', type: 'string', validation: Rule => Rule.required().max(60) },
    { name: 'excerpt', type: 'string', validation: Rule => Rule.required().max(160) },
    { name: 'mainImage', type: 'image', options: { hotspot: true },
      fields: [{ name: 'alt', type: 'string', validation: Rule => Rule.required() }] },
    { name: 'author', type: 'reference', to: [{ type: 'author' }] },
    { name: 'datePublished', type: 'datetime' },
    { name: 'jsonLdType', type: 'string',
      options: { list: ['Article', 'NewsArticle', 'BlogPosting', 'HowTo', 'Recipe'] } },
  ]
}

Content-Type with SEO component

Strapi schema
編集場所 src/api/article/content-types/article/schema.json
{
  "kind": "collectionType",
  "collectionName": "articles",
  "attributes": {
    "title": { "type": "string", "required": true },
    "slug": { "type": "uid", "targetField": "title", "required": true },
    "content": { "type": "richtext" },
    "seo": {
      "type": "component",
      "component": "shared.seo",
      "required": true
    }
  }
}

// src/components/shared/seo.json
{
  "attributes": {
    "metaTitle": { "type": "string", "required": true, "maxLength": 60 },
    "metaDescription": { "type": "string", "required": true, "maxLength": 160 },
    "shareImage": { "type": "media", "allowedTypes": ["images"] },
    "structuredData": { "type": "json" },
    "canonicalURL": { "type": "string" }
  }
}

Sitemap endpoint

SvelteKit indexing
編集場所 src/routes/sitemap.xml/+server.ts
export async function GET() {
  const posts = await getAllPosts()
  const xml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">
  ${posts.map(p => `
    <url>
      <loc>https://example.com/${p.slug}</loc>
      <lastmod>${p.updatedAt}</lastmod>
    </url>`).join('')}
</urlset>`
  return new Response(xml, { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/xml' } })
}
期待効果 +server.ts で動的 sitemap 配信
注意 50K URL 超は sitemap_index.xml で分割
📘 公式 doc

prerender for SSG

SvelteKit indexing
編集場所 src/routes/blog/[slug]/+page.ts
export const prerender = true

export async function load({ params }) {
  const post = await getPost(params.slug)
  return { post }
}

// + src/routes/blog/[slug]/+page.server.ts
export const entries = async () => {
  const posts = await getAllPosts()
  return posts.map(p => ({ slug: p.slug }))
}
期待効果 prerender = true でビルド時生成、static export 可能
注意 entries で dynamic route の全パスを列挙

Rank Math for technical SEO

WordPress schema
編集場所 wp-config.php / settings
// Rank Math automatically handles:
// - sitemap.xml + image sitemap + news sitemap
// - JSON-LD Schema (Article/Product/Recipe/HowTo/FAQ/Person/Org)
// - 404 monitor + redirections
// - Schema generator for custom types

// Programmatic: rank_math_meta filter
add_filter('rank_math/json_ld', function($data, $jsonld) {
    if (is_single()) {
        $data['CustomSchema'] = [
            '@type' => 'TechArticle',
            'proficiencyLevel' => 'Beginner',
            // ...
        ];
    }
    return $data;
}, 99, 2);
期待効果 Rank Math は Yoast 競合、無料で多機能
注意 Both Yoast + Rank Math 同時使用は不可
📘 公式 doc

Yoast SEO programmatic

WordPress schema
編集場所 functions.php
// Add custom Schema.org Article fields
add_filter('wpseo_schema_article', function($data, $context) {
    $data['author'] = [
        '@type' => 'Person',
        'name' => get_the_author(),
        'sameAs' => [
            get_user_meta(get_the_author_meta('ID'), 'orcid', true),
            get_user_meta(get_the_author_meta('ID'), 'wikidata_qid', true),
        ]
    ];
    return $data;
}, 10, 2);

// Add Featured Snippet trigger paragraph
add_action('the_content', function($content) {
    if (is_single() && !preg_match('/<p[^>]*>.{40,80}<\/p>/', $content)) {
        // First paragraph not optimal length - flag for editor
    }
    return $content;
});
期待効果 Yoast SEO filter で Schema を programmatically 拡張
注意 Yoast Premium が前提、free でも可能だが拡張少ない
📘 公式 doc
